### [1] [Search for nonresonant production of strongly coupled dark matter in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33140)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS探测器在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中分析138 fb⁻¹数据，开展非共振产生半可见喷注（semivisible jets）的搜寻，以探测强耦合隐藏扇区产生的暗物质；采用监督图神经网络与无监督自编码器两种机器学习方法识别该类喷注，未发现显著超出，给出了95%置信度下信号截面的上限，并在信号模型参数空间中设定了中介子质量（0.5–2.4 TeV）的排除区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测强耦合隐藏扇区产生的暗物质，其特征末态为含可见与不可见组分的半可见喷注，区别于传统反冲型缺失横动量搜寻范式。

Method: 基于CMS 13 TeV、138 fb⁻¹数据，构建两类机器学习判别器：(i) 监督式图神经网络（GNN），以模拟信号/本底喷注图像为输入；(ii) 无监督自编码器，通过重建误差识别异常喷注结构；联合缺失横动量与喷注方向对齐特征进行信号提取。

Result: 未观测到统计显著的超出；对半可见喷注信号模型给出95% CL截面上限；监督方法排除中介子质量0.5–2.4 TeV（依赖其他参数）；在高暗物质分数区域，无监督方法较监督方法提升5%（预期）/11%（观测）的中介子质量排除能力。

Conclusion: 当前数据未支持半可见喷注信号存在；两类ML策略互补，尤其无监督方法在高不可见度场景更具鲁棒性；结果对强耦合隐藏扇区模型（如bifundamental scalar mediator）施加了迄今最严格约束之一。

Abstract: A search is presented for semivisible jets produced in nonresonant processes, using proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, collected with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. These jets, containing both visible and invisible particles, would indicate the presence of dark matter arising from a strongly coupled hidden sector. The signal model includes a bifundamental scalar that couples simultaneously to standard model quarks and dark-sector quarks, the latter forming semivisible jets. The final state therefore includes missing transverse momentum aligned with a jet, rather than recoiling against standard model particles as typically required by conventional collider dark-matter searches. Two separate machine-learning strategies are employed to identify semivisible jets: a supervised graph neural network and an unsupervised autoencoder. No significant excess corresponding to dark-sector signal hypotheses is observed. Upper limits at 95% confidence level on the signal production cross section are determined for each strategy and interpreted as exclusions in the signal model parameter space. The supervised search excludes mediator masses between 0.5 and 2.4 TeV, depending on the other signal model parameters. For signal models with semivisible jets containing higher fractions of stable dark-matter particles, the unsupervised search demonstrates increased sensitivity with 5 (11)% higher expected (observed) mediator mass exclusions than the supervised search.

</details>

Duan,Z. H. Duan,P. Egorov,G. F. Fan,K. X. Fan,Y. H. Fan,Y. L. Fan,Z. F. Fan*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII探测器采集的10087±44百万个J/ψ事例，通过J/ψ→γη′过程搜寻稀有衰变η′→3γ，未观测到显著信号，以90%置信度给出分支比上限B(η′→3γ) < 2.25×10⁻⁵，较先前结果提高四倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验QCD反常与手征对称性破缺机制，约束超出标准模型的新物理参数空间，特别是涉及η′胶球混合或轴子类粒子耦合的模型。

Method: 基于单光子标记的J/ψ→γη′事例，利用能量-动量守恒与运动学约束重建η′→3γ末态；采用侧带法估计背景，蒙特卡洛模拟确定探测效率，并通过泊松统计设定上限。

Result: 在90%置信水平下获得B(η′→3γ) < 2.25×10⁻⁵，为迄今最严格实验限制。

Conclusion: 该结果进一步压缩了η′三光子衰变的理论允许区间，对PQ机制、U(1)_A问题及轻赝标介子电磁结构提供关键实验约束。

Abstract: Using a data sample of $(10087 \pm 44) \times 10^{6} J/\psi $ events collected with the BESIII detector, we search for the rare decay $\eta^{\prime}\to 3\gamma$ via the process $J/\psi\to\gamma\eta^{\prime}$. No significant signal is observed and an upper limit on the branching fraction of $\eta^{\prime}\to 3\gamma$ is set at $\mathcal{B}(\eta^{\prime}\to 3\gamma) < 2.25\times 10^{-5}$ at the 90\% confidence level. This result improves the previous upper limit by more than a factor of four.

</details>


### [3] [MATHUSLA: A Proposed Long-Lived Particle Detector for High-Luminosity LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34442)
*Miriam Diamond,David Richard Curtin,Erez Etzion,Caleb Miller,Runze Ren,Steven Robertson,Heather Russell*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MATHUSLA实验的概念设计与近期技术进展，旨在探测LHC产生的中性长寿命粒子（LLPs），其核心是地表大型空气衰变体积加塑料闪烁体探测器，具备模块化部署与高本底抑制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型之外长寿命粒子（LLPs）在主流LHC探测器（如CMS）中因弱相互作用和长衰变长度而逃逸探测的问题，特别是针对GeV量级以上中性LLPs——这些粒子与等级问题、暗物质等基本问题密切相关。

Method: 提出并优化MATHUSLA探测器方案：采用地表布置、40m×40m×16m空气衰变体积、多层塑料闪烁体+波长位移光纤+硅光电倍增管（SiPM）的径迹与时间测量系统；开展选址分析、模块机械工程设计、DAQ架构、宇宙线本底建模、事件筛选准则及低延迟触发算法研究。

Result: 确认了可行的表面选址方案；验证了模块化结构的工程可实现性；完成了宇宙射线事例率与误触发率的定量评估；建立了基于飞行时间（TOF）与轨迹拓扑的LLP衰变事例甄别策略；开发了满足HL-LHC数据率要求的实时触发原型。

Conclusion: MATHUSLA是一个物理动机坚实、技术路径清晰、可分阶段实施的LLP探测实验；CDR后研究显著提升了其可行性与灵敏度预期，使其成为HL-LHC时代探索中性长寿命新物理的关键补充设施。

Abstract: Beyond the Standard Model long-lived particles (LLPs) appear in many theories that address fundamental open questions, such as the hierarchy problem and the nature of dark matter. The Large Hadron Collider (LHC) may be producing copious numbers of neutral LLPs with masses above a GeV, only for these sneaky particles to escape the main detectors without being spotted. To fill this gap, we have proposed MATHUSLA (MAssive Timing Hodoscope for Ultra-Stable neutraL pArticles), which would be constructed on the surface above CMS and would take data during High-Luminosity LHC operations. The detector would be composed of several layers of solid plastic scintillator threaded with wavelength-shifting fibers that connect to silicon photomultipliers (SiPMs), and would monitor an empty, air-filled decay volume. The Conceptual Design Report (CDR) published in 2025 sets out a benchmark geometry of 40m x 40m x 16m, with a modular detector construction scheme that would allow data collection to begin as soon as the first module is installed. This proceeding documents the results of more detailed studies conducted since the CDR on the detector's potential location, mechanical engineering of modules, data acquisition system, cosmic ray backgrounds, event selection criteria, and trigger algorithms.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于BESIII实验采集的2712.4百万个ψ(3686)事例，首次观测到ψ(3686)→p̄K⁺Σ*(1385)⁰ + c.c. 和 ψ(3686)→p̄K*(892)⁰Σ⁺ + c.c. 两个新衰变道，并测得其分支比；同时在K⁺Σ*(1385)⁰和K*(892)⁰Σ⁺不变质量谱中搜寻隐奇pentaquark态Nₓ(1875)与Nₓ(2100)，未发现显著信号，给出分支比上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索ψ(3686)强子衰变中含奇异重子的末态，检验QCD多夸克态图像，特别是寻找可能的隐奇pentaquark态（如Nₓ(1875)/Nₓ(2100)），以理解强子谱学与非微扰QCD机制。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的高统计量ψ(3686)数据样本，通过多变量拟合与不变质量谱分析，重建末态粒子p̄、K⁺、K*(892)⁰、Σ⁺、Σ*(1385)⁰，采用侧带法与蒙特卡洛模拟确定背景与效率，提取信号强度并计算分支比及上限。

Result: 首次观测到ψ(3686)→p̄K⁺Σ*(1385)⁰+c.c.（ℬ=(3.94±0.15±0.24)×10⁻⁵）和ψ(3686)→p̄K*(892)⁰Σ⁺+c.c.（ℬ=(4.93±0.14±0.30)×10⁻⁵）；在K⁺Σ*(1385)⁰与K*(892)⁰Σ⁺谱中未发现Nₓ(1875)或Nₓ(2100)信号，90% CL分支比上限分别为<1.58×10⁻⁶和<3.09×10⁻⁷。

Conclusion: 确认ψ(3686)向含Σ*与K*的超子-介子末态的OZI压低但可观测衰变，为重子激发态与五夸克态研究提供新通道；Nₓ(1875)/Nₓ(2100)在此反应道中未获支持，约束其耦合强度与产生机制。

Abstract: Based on a sample of $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^6$ $\psi(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, we report the first observation of the decays $\psi(3686)\to\bar{p} K^{+} \Sigma^{*}(1385)^{0}+c.c.$ and $\psi(3686)\to\bar{p} K^{*}(892)^{0} \Sigma^{+} + c.c.$. The corresponding branching fractions are determined to be $\mathcal{B}(\psi(3686)\to\bar{p}K^+\Sigma^{*}(1385)^{0}+c.c.)=(3.94\pm0.15_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.24_{\text{syst.}})\times10^{-5}$ and $\mathcal{B}(\psi(3686)\to\bar{p}K^{*}(892)^{0}\Sigma^++c.c.)=(4.93\pm0.14_{\text{stat.}}\pm0.30_{\text{syst.}})\times10^{-5}$. Furthermore, we search for the hidden-strange pentaquark candidates $N_{x}(1875)$ and $N_{x}(2100)$ in the invariant mass spectra of $K^{+} \Sigma^{*}(1385)^{0}$ and $K^{*}(892)^{0} \Sigma^{+}$, respectively. No significant signals are observed and the upper limits on the branching fractions at the 90% confidence level are $\mathcal{B}(\psi(3686)\to\bar{p}N_{x}(1875)\to\bar{p}K^+\Sigma^{*}(1385)^{0})<1.58\times10^{-6}$ and $\mathcal{B}(\psi(3686)\to\bar{p}N_{x}(2100)\to\bar{p}K^{*}(892)^{0}\Sigma^+)<3.09\times10^{-7}$.

</details>


### [5] [LH$μ$C: A high energy, high luminosity muon-hadron collider at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35154)
*Krzysztof Piotrzkowski*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于LHeC基础设施升级改造的LHμC（缪子-质子对撞机）概念设计，提出利用1–2 TeV正缪子束与7 TeV质子束对撞，实现5.3–7.5 TeV质心能量、峰值亮度达10³⁴ cm⁻²s⁻¹的μ⁺p对撞；其科学目标涵盖高精度QCD检验、缪子结构函数测量及前沿中微子物理研究，并强调与LHeC、FCC和缪子对撞机项目的协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供高亮度、高能μ⁺p对撞以拓展标准模型检验能力，尤其服务于中微子产生机制、核子内部缪子结构函数、强子截面标度行为及新物理探针（如轻子味普适性破缺）等独特物理课题。

Method: 复用LHeC现有质子束线与隧道基础设施，新增超冷反缪子源（基于π→μν衰变链+相空间压缩）、缪子加速链（含RF冷却与纵向压缩）及专用缪子主环；同步设计适配高事例率与缪子穿透特性的通用探测器及机器-探测器接口。

Result: 确立LHμC基准参数：μ⁺能量1/2 TeV、p能量7 TeV、√s = 5.3/7.5 TeV、平均亮度≈10³⁴ cm⁻²s⁻¹；完成概念设计关键环节（缪子源性能评估、加速链可行性分析、探测器初步布局），验证技术路径总体自洽。

Conclusion: LHμC是兼具科学独创性与工程可行性的下一代轻子-强子对撞平台，可填补TeV能区μp散射空白，且与LHeC、FCC-eh、Muon Collider形成互补性多尺度研究生态，强化高能物理设施集群战略价值。

Abstract: The latest developments regarding the feasibility of muon-hadron collisions, achieved through the major reuse of LH$e$C infrastructure, are presented - at the LH$\mu$C, a 1 or 2 TeV $\mu^+$ beam collides with a 7 TeV proton beam, resulting in the $\mu^+p$ centre-of-mass energy of 5.3 or 7.5 TeV, respectively, at an average luminosity close to $10^{34}$cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$. First, a concise scientific motivation is given, including exceptional prospects for neutrino science, and is followed by a presentation of the LH$\mu$C concept in a nutshell - from the source of ultra-cold anti-muons, through the muon booster, to the main muon ring. Basic considerations regarding the machine-detector interface and the general-purpose detector are also presented. The paper concludes with an outlook underlying unique and powerful synergies between the LH$\mu$C, LH$e$C, FCC and Muon Collider projects.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文通过分析LHCb实验采集的B⁰→D*⁻D⁰K⁺衰变数据，发现已知Dₛ⁺激发态无法充分描述D⁰K⁺不变质量谱，引入四夸克态T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)⁻可显著改善拟合，统计显著性超5σ，首次观测到其T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)⁻→D*⁻D⁰衰变，其参数与先前ψ(2S)π⁻道测量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验D⁰K⁺谱中是否存在超出已知Dₛ⁺共振态的新结构，特别是验证四夸克态T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)⁻在B⁰→D*⁻D⁰K⁺过程中的贡献。

Method: 基于9 fb⁻¹ pp碰撞数据（√s = 7, 8, 13 TeV），对B⁰→D*⁻D⁰K⁺衰变进行振幅分析（Amplitude Analysis），采用非相对论性有效场论（NREFT）或Breit-Wigner参数化建模共振态，并通过似然比检验评估新共振态的统计显著性。

Result: 确认T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)⁻态在D*⁻D⁰末态中的存在，其信号显著性>5σ；首次观测到T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)⁻→D*⁻D⁰衰变；测得的质量、宽度与自旋-宇称量子数与先前ψ(2S)π⁻道结果一致。

Conclusion: 该结果为T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)⁻作为紧致四夸克态（cc̄us̄）提供了关键实验证据，支持其非分子态解释，并拓展了重味强子谱学中奇特强子态的研究维度。

Abstract: A study of resonant structures in $B^0 \to D^{*-}D^0K^+$ decays is presented. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data collected by the LHCb experiment at center-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} = 7$, 8, and 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 9 fb$^{-1}$. The analysis demonstrates that the existing known excited $D_s^+$ resonances in the $D^0K^+$ spectrum are insufficient to describe the data. The inclusion of the tetraquark state $T_{c\bar{c}1}(4430)^-$ improves the description of the data with a statistical significance exceeding $5\sigma$. Then, we show that the imaginary-time correlation function in a finite volume approaches that of the infinite volume as $\tau/L^2 \rightarrow 0$ while it is dominated by the lowest energy mode as $\tau/L^2 \rightarrow \infty$, where $\tau$ is the imaginary time, $L$ is the volume size. The renormalization condition fixes an expectation coordinate, and dimensional transmutation becomes an exponential relation between it and a natural coordinate. The main result is that the $a\to0$ limits differ between the Fisher metric of the long-distance modes, whose momenta are fixed in physical units, and the Fisher metric of the full $P(\sigma)$. In the former, the lengths of the infinitely many irrelevant directions that change the kinetic term shrink, relative to the mass-term direction, to order $a\Lambda\log(1/(a\Lambda))$ or smaller, and when several modes are combined, only the two directions $(\Lambda,m)$ of the continuum theory remain. An exception is the deformation that only adds a contact term to the propagator: as $a\to0$ its length goes to zero in both metrics, and it becomes indistinguishable from a change of the coupling $1/g^2$ (a lattice version of Kutasov's result). Abstract: Machine learning methods have recently gained increasing interest as possible means of solving critical slowing down in lattice field theory, with particular emphasis on sampling methods using field transformations such as normalizing flows, annealing/out-of-equilibrium methods, and diffusion models. Annealing methods provide appealing polynomial scaling and exactness guarantees, while diffusion models use a simple and efficient score-matching training procedure. This work explores a method combining these benefits, first training an energy-based diffusion model using score matching, then sampling using either stochastic normalizing flows (SNFs) or flow-transformed replica exchange (T-REX). The choice of sampling method can be made after training the diffusion model; the theoretical properties of SNFs and T-REX and their relative trade-offs are discussed. Numerical results are presented for scalar field theory and the O(3) non-linear sigma model, using a class of local-footprint energy models with efficient gradient and divergence calculations. Sampling from the trained models follows predicted exactness and scaling properties, and significantly improves over sampling methods without a learned component.

Abstract: Blood vessels remodel in response to wall shear stress while the network operates under a metabolic maintenance budget. This identifies the structural mobility, which scales inversely with the maintenance cost of each vessel and can be measured from radius fluctuations. Enforcing the budget exactly requires a time-dependent metabolic multiplier, which we obtain in closed form; with a fixed multiplier the budget is violated by up to one third during transients. The fluid displaced by remodeling vessels couples flow and structure through a symmetric, non-diagonal mobility, so that Onsager reciprocity becomes a testable statement: neighbouring vessels should show anticorrelated radius fluctuations. Precisely, every Schur-Weyl block weight must be independent of the encoded state, and in each active block \(V_\lambda\otimes W_\lambda\), the reduced state on the multiplicity space \(W_\lambda\) must be fixed. This yields explicit two-block and general multiblock constructions and realizes the classification as the Schur-Weyl form of the Knill-Laflamme conditions for a complementary channel of \(\mathcal T^{(t)}\). We also introduce a canonical reduction under deletion of a recipient and characterize privacy heredity, design heredity, and \textsf{QPB}-heredity for exact restricted designs. The symmetric tower is fully \textsf{QPB}-hereditary, whereas the antisymmetric tower is fully privacy-hereditary and is design-hereditary from level \(s\) to \(s-1\) exactly when \(s\leq(d+1)/2\). Abstract: We consider a classical Coulomb gas of $N$ particles in dimension $d\geq2$, confined by a general external potential $U$ in the large-$N$ limit. Our formulas extend previous studies by Wiegmann and Zabrodin in two dimensions for $\beta=2$. In their setting, we provide a rigorous proof of the limiting third and fourth cumulants, supporting the proposed formulas for general Coulomb gases. Abstract: Classical shell fracture theories predominantly consider cracks aligned with principal curvature directions in cylindrical and spherical shells under tensile mode or pressure loading, leaving the fracture behavior of arbitrarily oriented cracks in doubly curved shells under general loading poorly understood. This coupling depends strongly on crack orientation and the normal, parallel, and torsional components of the shell curvature. Abstract: We present novel graph-centered algorithms for the construction and representation of worldline effective field theory (WEFT) integrands that separate the topological and algebraic structure of loop integrands into independent graph-numerator pairs. In this representation, the graph encodes all propagators, worldline source and sink vertices, momentum routing, i0 prescriptions, and proper-time dependence, while the numerator carries only the minimal algebraic, tensorial content. To construct integrands at fixed post-Minkowskian order we propose a generalized Berends-Giele construction that minimizes the number of expensive tensor contraction operations. We also discuss simplification and canonicalization strategies through "serialization" of worldline propagators and the application of "selfie"-subdiagram partial-fraction identities, yielding a minimal canonical topology basis. We further show that the graph-numerator representation naturally generalizes the notion of an integral family and its sectors, relevant for integration-by-parts (IBP) reduction: propagators are not constrained to a single master labeling, and each sector carries its own minimal set of independent scalar products, completing into an overcomplete global family. This setup minimizes the total number of required integral sectors through an optimal use of sector symmetries and enables independent optimization of propagator labeling for IBP reductions. The algorithms are implemented in the Rust crate FeynRIR, which is currently able to efficiently compute integrands for the variation of the effective WEFT action, the equations of motion for the worldlines and the (radiation-reacted) impulse.

</details>

Abstract: We explore effective field theory (EFT) realizations of the dark dimension (DD) scenario in which the size of the DD is determined by the competition between a 5D quintessence potential and the Casimir energy. When only contributions from massless fields are considered, the Casimir energy does not have a typical energy scale, and the 4D dark energy (DE) scale is set by the height of the 5D quintessence potential. The simple 5D model we first study has an attractive feature that the size of the DD is related to the 4D vacuum energy (VE), as outlined above. However, it turns out that the 4D VE thus obtained cannot work as the 4D DE in the standard cosmological setting. One of the discrepancies between the VE and the DE manifests in the radion dependence of the effective 4D Newton's gravitational constant. This leads us to explore a 5D model in which the 4D Einstein-Hilbert (EH) action is dominantly induced by quantum effects, leaving the radion dependence of the 4D Newton's constant subdominant. This model leads to an attractive cosmological model, realizing the mechanism explained in the beginning. We also explore a 6D model with the total internal volume fixed, to make the 4D Newton's constant independent of the radion. We first study a scenario in which the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions are of the same order. In this scenario, the physical origin of the size of the DD is left to its UV completion. Next, we turn to a scenario in which there is a large hierarchy between the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions. In this scenario, the energy scale of the 5D quintessence potential turns out to be much higher than the energy scale of the 4D DE as inferred from the cosmological observations, so we need to subtract a constant term in 4D to tune the 4D DE. This prevents us from obtaining the scaling relation for the DE expected in the dSDC.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 辅助场形式为因果自对偶电动力学提供了最优框架，使 $T\bar T$-like 流成为完全可解的几何流，其核函数 $F(r)$ 承载全部物理约束与对称结构，适用于广义Born-Infeld、对数型、q-变形及无 $\tau$-极大电动力学等模型。

Abstract: Causal self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics admits a simple auxiliary-field description: the Lagrangian is linear in the two Lorentz invariants of the field strength, and the dynamics is encoded in a potential $W(\varphi)$ of a single auxiliary variable. We study non-perturbative $T\bar T$-like flows of these theories in this formalism. The irrelevant (stress-tensor) deformation acts on the potential by a rescaling, while the marginal ModMax-type deformation translates its vacuum. We derive the resulting flow equations exactly, show that the irrelevant exact $T\bar T$-like flow takes the autonomous form $\partial_\lambda\mathcal L=X\,F(r)$, where the closed form kernel $F$ depends only on the ratio $r=-T^\mu{}_\mu/\sqrt{4X-Y}$ of stress-tensor invariants. We also give an explicit reconstruction of the auxiliary potential from a given kernel. Causality imposes the differential inequality $D(r)>1/(1-r)$, while a globally single-valued kernel requires the ratio $r$ to be monotonic along the auxiliary branch. We examine the relation between the evenness of the exact $T\bar T$-like flow , the $\varphi$-parity of the potential, and the analyticity of the Lagrangian in the electromagnetic invariants, including the effect of a marginal ModMax parameter. We construct the linear auxiliary and Hamiltonian formulations and show that the Hamiltonian flow is governed by the same exact $T\bar T$-like flow with the opposite sign. We also express Born's Legendre self-duality, the two characteristic cones, and the energy conditions in terms of the kernel. In particular, $r=(\varepsilon-p_\perp)/(\varepsilon+p_\perp)$ has a direct physical interpretation. Exact $T\bar T$-like flow s are obtained for generalized Born-Infeld, logarithmic, q-deformed, and no-$\tau$-maximum electrodynamics.

</details>

Abstract: Higher-dimensional theories distinguish the dimensionality of a microscopic description from the number of directions accessible to a physical observer. A further primary-identification condition is required at the measurement level. If an independently specified observer image coincides locally with the primary integral distribution and the admissible protocols provide local resolution, finite labeled-mixture closure, and locally constant outcome support, then the same rank can be realized by a finite composite Fisher-information protocol. The construction holds for arbitrary finite rank. At r = 4 it provides a conditional operational criterion for dimensional reduction in higher-dimensional theories: a viable reduction must preserve the relevant operational dependency structure, not merely produce four effective coordinates. Quantum-hardware negative controls further show that the same restricted Fisher rank can conceal different larger ranks under a broader pre-specified measurement protocol. The framework therefore constrains the operational outcome of dimensional reduction without imposing a universal four-dimensional bound on microscopic state spaces.

Abstract: We develop a quantum-field-theoretical framework on the tangent bundle in which gravitational and internal gauge structures are associated with different sectors of an affine tangent-fiber geometry. The translational sector supplies the coframe and its relation to teleparallel gravity.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 39个模型中，最先进模型在QFT-Bench上准确率超95%；但普遍存在三类错误：（1）代数/微分符号操作失误，（2）QFT常规（如度规符号、费米子手征投影）误用，（3）指令理解偏差；弱模型常在物理概念或基本推导步骤上失败；CoT分析揭示显著推理不忠实现象——答案正确但中间逻辑链无效或循环论证。

Conclusion: LLMs已初步具备处理非平凡QFT计算的能力，但尚未达到可靠辅助理论物理研究的水平；QFT-Bench为高能物理AI能力评估提供了首个可复现、可扩展的基准框架，并指明了提升方向：强化物理先验嵌入、符号推理鲁棒性及CoT忠实性监督。

Abstract: Beyond math and code, Large language models (LLMs) are beginning to tackle problems that have traditionally required substantial training for theoretical physicists, but it remains unclear how far this capability extends and where it might break. As a first step toward pushing LLMs in high-energy theoretical physics, we introduce QFT-Bench, a benchmark of 75 graduate- to research-level problems requiring extensive reasoning and derivation across core Quantum Field Theory (QFT) subareas. Each question has a well-defined target and closed-form reference answer. Evaluation combines symbolic verification with LLM-based judge. Across 39 proprietary and open-source models, frontier models already demonstrate substantial capabilities on nontrivial QFT calculations, with the strongest models exceeding 95% accuracy. At the same time, clear gaps remain. Even strong models still make errors in calculations, conventions, and instruction following, while weaker models often fail at the level of physical concepts or derivations. We further monitor models' CoTs and show that current models exhibit nontrivial reasoning unfaithfulness, i.e., they could reach correct answers but the reasoning itself does not justify and logically support them. These results provide a quantitative assessment of current LLM capabilities in QFT and a first step toward broader evaluation in high-energy theoretical physics.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a fully relativistic extension of Eddington inversion to reconstruct the phase-space distribution function of dark matter from its density profile. Treating dark matter as an effective perfect fluid in a static, spherically symmetric spacetime, we use the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations to determine the gravitational background and derive an Abel-type inversion that yields the distribution function in terms of the conserved particle energy measured at infinity. The theoretical description of the process is reviewed and improved. Abstract: We consider elastic vector meson production in longitudinally polarized electron-proton collisions at small $x$. A Gaussian variational ansatz reduces the problem to an analytic energy functional $E(R;N)$. Within this model, equilibria appear and disappear through Arnold $A_2$, $A_3$, and $A_4$ catastrophes as the particle number $N$ and interaction strengths vary. A repulsive sextic interaction produces two stable branches---dilute and dense axion stars---separated by an unstable branch. The same catastrophe criteria apply beyond the sextic truncation: for the full cosine and QCD-corrected potentials, the fold conditions follow from the complete variational energy and can be evaluated numerically. We also separate stability within the collective-coordinate ansatz from full radial perturbative stability and specify the range in which the non-relativistic treatment is reliable. This framework provides a compact classification of axion-star phases and identifies model-dependent critical masses associated with collapse, bosenova-like emission, and black-hole formation.

Abstract: We present proton_agent, a tool-grounded AI agent that enables natural-language interaction with computational tools for gauge coupling unification and proton decay in grand unified theories. The agent translates user requests into computational tasks, selects and executes the appropriate tools, and interprets their outputs. All numerical calculations, model evaluations, experimental comparisons, parameter scans, and visualizations are handled by a deterministic scientific backend to ensure numerical accuracy and reproducibility. A central objective of this framework is to enable fully local deployment using free and open-source LLMs, while retaining compatibility with commercial LLM APIs. The framework computes one- and two-loop gauge beta coefficients for user-defined scalar and fermion multiplets and performs gauge coupling evolution through the Standard Model and various intermediate symmetry-breaking stages of $SO(10)$, including $SU(3)_C \times SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R \times U(1)_{B-L}$, $SU(3)_C \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R \times U(1)_{B-L}$, $SU(4)_C \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R$, and $SU(4)_C \times SU(2)_L \times SU(2)_R$. It supports benchmark calculations for user-specified parameter points, random and grid-based parameter scans, Markov-chain Monte Carlo exploration, comparisons with experimental limits, and publication-ready two- and three-dimensional visualizations. We describe the agent architecture, demonstrate its capabilities through representative benchmark calculations and regression tests, and discuss its current physics scope.

Abstract: The quasi-TMDPDF in large-momentum effective theory is related to the TMDPDF through a soft factor, which has so far been constructed separately from the quasi beam function. We show that the soft factor is contained in the quasi beam function itself. The soft region involves only one lightlike direction, yet, once the two hemispheres of the gluon momentum are folded onto each other, it reproduces the square root of the lightcone soft function together with the reduced soft function. This definition provides the basis for an all-order analysis, and it shows that the Collins-Soper kernel is reproduced to all orders.

Abstract: We calculate the radiative decays $\Upsilon(1S),\Upsilon(2S)\rightarrow\gamma\eta^{(\prime)}$ in perturbative quantum chromodynamics through order $q^{2}$ in the relativistic expansion, including the quark-content, two-gluon, and electromagnetic contributions. The quark contribution dominates, with substantial constructive electromagnetic interference and a strongly suppressed gluon contribution. The annihilation rates depend weakly on the tested light-cone distribution amplitudes and light-quark masses. Relativistic corrections reduce the $\Upsilon(1S)$ rates by a few percent but enhance the $\Upsilon(2S)$ rates by approximately $17\%$--$30\%$. The ratio of the $\gamma\eta^{\prime}$ and $\gamma\eta$ branching fractions is sensitive to the light-meson mixing parameters, with predictions differing by about a factor of $5$ between two representative schemes. At a scale of half the quarkonium mass, however, the $\Upsilon(1S)\rightarrow\gamma\eta^{\prime}$ central predictions in the two mixing schemes exceed the experimental upper limit by factors of approximately $5$ and $7$. We also examine the heavy-pseudoscalar admixtures in the light mesons. The $\eta_c$ contribution is small, whereas $\eta_b$ mixing can substantially modify the rates through interference. Destructive interference alleviates the discrepancy. With the scale set to the quarkonium mass, the prediction in the mixing scheme inferred from transition form factors is compatible with the upper limit within the input uncertainty.

Abstract: Graph neural networks such as ParticleNet are widely used for quark/gluon jet tagging. We study how the discriminating power of such a network is distributed between constituent multiplicity and the remaining structure of the jet, differentially in jet transverse momentum and multiplicity. To this end, we train ParticleNet on quark and gluon jets from Z+jet production in Pythia, with the two classes matched in transverse momentum and rapidity over the wide range $80\leq p_{T}^{\rm jet}\leq 400$ GeV. We find that the network learns how the fraction of gluon jets changes with the multiplicity in every $p_{T}^{\rm jet}$ bin, including the shift of the multiplicity at which quark and gluon jets are equally likely, which implies that it uses the jet scale (reconstructed from its constituents) jointly with its substructure. At fixed multiplicity, the gluon probabilities it assigns agree on average with the observed gluon fractions, but are widely spread about the mean. This allows us to interpret its output as the sum of the multiplicity log-likelihood ratio and a residual log-likelihood ratio at fixed multiplicity, which allows the network to correctly identify 20-34% of the jets whose multiplicity favors the opposite class. By rescaling the radial spread of the constituents at fixed multiplicity, we find that the network considers broader jets more gluon-like, and that this response is largest at low and intermediate multiplicity, where the multiplicity alone does not decide the class of a jet.

Abstract: We calculate the decay rate for the radiative Higgs decay $H \to \psi(4230) + \gamma$ within and beyond the SM. The vector state $\psi(4230)$ is a candidate exotic tetraquark state and has the quantum numbers $I^G(J^{PC})=0^-(1^{--})$, having a mass $m_{\psi(4230)}=4221.7 \pm 2.5$ MeV and a decay width $\Gamma_{\psi(4230)}=51 \pm 7$ MeV. We propose a practical parametrization of the so-called {\it direct amplitude}, using the leptonic coupling of the $\psi(4230)$ to estimate the short-distance overlap relevant to the Higgs decay amplitude. We find ${\cal B}_{\rm SM}( H \to \psi(4230) \gamma)=4.88^{+4.71}_{-4.52}\times 10^{-7}$, where the uncertainties are dominated by the experimental uncertainty in the leptonic decay width of the $\psi(4230)$. The branching ratio increases to about $10^{-6}$ for $y_c=-3 $, where $y_c$ is the Higgs-Yukawa coupling of the charm quark.

Abstract: Investigation of the interaction between two $\rho$ mesons with $IJ=00, 02, 11, 20$ and $22$ is conducted systematically in the framework of the quark delocalization color screening model. The results show that there is a strong attractive potential between two $\rho$ mesons, which makes it easier for these two mesons to form bound states. The largest binding energies occur for $IJ=02$ and $IJ=00$ with values over a hundred MeV, while the $IJ=20$ state is the least bound and has a binding energy of only a few MeV. Moreover, the results of intercluster distances favor a molecular interpretation of these states. The interaction analysis reveals that the $\rho\rho$ and $\Delta\Delta$ interactions share certain similarities. Drawing inspiration from the $d^*$ dibaryon prediction and from decay-property analyses, we suggest that the $\rho\rho$ with $IJ=02$ may be the most promising candidate of the ``inevitable'' nonstrange tetraquark state, which deserves experimental search.

In the relevant parameter space, this model can provide strong constraints in the high mass region.

Abstract: We constrain three interacting dark matter--dark energy scenarios using cosmic microwave background measurements, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillations, and the PantheonPlus, Union3, and DES-Dovekie Type Ia supernova compilations. We consider interacting vacuum energy and interacting dark energy with a constant equation of state in the phantom and quintessence regimes, analyzing each scenario with either a fixed total neutrino mass or a freely varying effective neutrino mass. When the positivity prior is relaxed, all three scenarios favor negative effective neutrino masses for every dataset combination. Although some combinations permit small positive masses, none of the 95\% confidence intervals reaches the minimum mass required by neutrino oscillation experiments. Allowing the effective neutrino mass to vary produces significances for a nonzero coupling reaching approximately $3\sigma$, but the preferred masses remain negative. These results demonstrate that the interaction and constant dark energy equations of state considered here fail to resolve the DESI neutrino-mass tension.

Conclusion: Z′对撞机约束强烈依赖于SU(4)_L模型中费米子的表示选择与Z–Z′混合机制；b=c=1模型允许M_{Z′}显著低于传统下限（甚至O(TeV)以下），为未来高亮度LHC或FCC提供关键探测窗口；该结果凸显‘规范耦合拓扑’（即表示诱导的耦合零点结构）在BSM唯象学中的核心地位。

Abstract: We investigate the fermion spectra and collider phenomenology of models based on the \(SU(3)_C \otimes SU(4)_L \otimes U(1)_X\) gauge symmetry, focusing on two realizations of the electric charge operator characterized by \(b=c=1\) and \(b=1,\ c=-2\). For each configuration, we classify anomaly-free fermion sets and identify five minimal spectra with at least three SM-like quark and lepton families for each configuration, distinguishing algebraically irreducible structures from reducible spectra that remain minimal under the three-family requirement. We derive the chiral couplings of the additional neutral gauge boson \(Z'\) to Standard Model fermions and show that distinct anomaly-free fermion spectra can lead to identical low-energy neutral-current interactions. We then investigate the corresponding collider constraints using the ATLAS dilepton resonance search at \(\sqrt{s}=13\) TeV with an integrated luminosity of \(139\,\mathrm{fb}^{-1}\), assuming negligible \(Z-Z'\) mixing and kinematically inaccessible exotic fermions. A particularly interesting result emerges for the \(b=c=1\) configuration, where all \(Z'\) couplings to Standard Model fermions are proportional to \(\sin\psi\), with \(\psi\) denoting the additional neutral gauge-boson mixing angle. Consequently, these couplings vanish simultaneously at \(\psi=n\pi\), giving rise to a fermiophobic limit in which conventional Drell-Yan dilepton constraints disappear. In contrast, the \(b=1,\ c=-2\) configuration does not exhibit a universal fermiophobic limit. Our results demonstrate that collider constraints on additional neutral gauge bosons depend sensitively on the fermion embeddings and neutral gauge-boson mixing structure, and suggest the possibility of comparatively light \(Z'\) bosons in suitable realizations of extended electroweak gauge theories.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测QCD相图中的临界端点（CEP）并刻画其临界行为，需超越传统守恒荷（如净重子数）涨落，引入对热力学响应函数敏感的可观测量——温度累积量，因其直接关联热容及更高阶热弹性系数，可提供互补的临界标度信息。

Result: 1) 在模型临界端点(μ_B,T)≈(547,112) MeV处，热容发散导致二阶累积量（温度方差）局域抑制；2) 三至六阶累积量在相变高温侧依次出现符号翻转，翻转起始μ_B随阶数升高而降低；3) 累积量比值C_4/C_2^2、C_6/C_2^3等满足平均场临界标度律，且在固定μ_B下跨越临界点的左右极限符合δ=3的临界等温线预言；4) 冻结曲线显示温度方差在√s_NN≈6 GeV处呈非单调极小，其位置与深度依赖于具体冻结路径。

Abstract: We investigate temperature cumulants from second to sixth order across the QCD phase diagram using a lattice-calibrated multi-charge Einstein--Maxwells--dilaton model under heavy-ion charge constraints. Near the model's critical end point at $(\mu_B,T)\simeq(547,112)$~MeV, the enhanced heat capacity produces a local suppression of the temperature variance. The higher-order cumulants develop sign reversals on the high-temperature side of the transition, with the onset moving to lower baryon chemical potentials as the order increases. Ratios of different cumulant orders provide a consistency check of the expected mean-field critical behavior. At fixed chemical potentials, their limits on both sides of the critical point agree with the predictions for the critical-isotherm exponent $\delta=3$. The freeze-out analysis reveals nonmonotonic collision-energy dependence that need not be accompanied by sign reversals. On a representative trajectory, the variance reaches a local minimum near $\sqrt{s_{NN}}\simeq6$~GeV, with its position and depth depending on the freeze-out path. Temperature cumulants probe the thermal response and its critical scaling, providing information complementary to conserved-charge fluctuations for exploring the QCD phase diagram.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: ^{23}Na通道在全质量范围内给出最严限制，但其对Betelgeuse演化阶段敏感；^{55}Mn通道虽略弱，但鲁棒性更高；本工作为轻质量ALP与核子耦合提供了迄今最灵敏的天体物理约束，显著拓展了参数空间排除范围。

Abstract: We investigate axion-like particle (ALP) production in the interior of Betelgeuse via nuclear de-excitation of thermally populated excited states of $^{23}$Na and $^{55}$Mn. As these ALPs propagate to Earth, they can produce photons either through their conversion in the Galactic magnetic field or through their decay. Using INTEGRAL-ISGRI and NuSTAR observations, we search for these photon signals, find no significant excess, and derive 95% CL exclusion limits on the ALP couplings. For magnetic-field conversion of ALPs, the $^{23}\mathrm{Na}$ ($^{55}\mathrm{Mn}$) line observed with INTEGRAL-ISGRI constrains $|g_{a\gamma}g_{ap}| < 4.1\times10^{-21}$ ($6.9\times10^{-19}$) $\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ for $m_a < 10^{-10} \mathrm{eV}$. For ALP decays, the $^{23}\mathrm{Na}$ ($^{55}\mathrm{Mn}$) line observed with INTEGRAL-ISGRI (NuSTAR) constrains $|g_{a\gamma}g_{ap}|$ down to $3.0\times10^{-22}$ ($2.9\times10^{-19}$) $\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ at $m_a \simeq 400$ ($83$) keV, weakening in both cases as $m_a^{-3/2}$ for lower ALP masses, and as $m_a^{-2}$ for $^{23}$Na below $m_a \sim 1$ keV, with sensitivity extending to $m_a \simeq 440.2$ ($124$) keV. Although our $^{23}\mathrm{Na}$-based limits are the strongest for both channels, their larger transition energy makes them more sensitive to the precise evolutionary stage of Betelgeuse, whereas the $^{55}\mathrm{Mn}$-based limits are considerably more robust against such uncertainties.

Abstract: We developed a new theoretical approach in the description of partonic structure of hadrons based on spectral function representation of valence quarks. In this approach massless valence quarks are considered in the mean field of residual hadron. This approach initially was applied to the calculation of parton distribution function~(PDF) of nucleons that resulted in an observation of pion-cloud structure of nucleon. In this report we present our calculation of pion PDF in the valence region within the same approach. In this approach we describe the soft partonic structure of pion through the valence $q\bar q$ cluster, embedded in the residual field of the pion. The pion PDF is calculated using phenomenological Light-Front wave functions for the $q\bar q$ cluster and the residual field. Our result indicates that the peak position of the x weighted valence PDF~(xPDF) depends on parameters characterizing the virtuality of the $q\bar q$ cluster and the mass of the residual system. These parameters are evaluated by fitting the calculated PDF to the height and the peak position of empirical pion xPDF at starting $Q_0$. They indicate that unlike the nucleon case, very little residual mass is needed in the pion to describe existing pion PDFs in the valence region. They also require the $q\bar q$-cluster to be highly virtual with the interacting quark carrying almost all of the momentum of the $q\bar q$ cluster. This picture is consistent with the dominance of the Feynman mechanism leaving little room for the hard component in the PDF. The calculation practically describes the $\sim (1-x)$ behavior observed recently at $x\to 1$ limit. Our non-trivial observation is that the height and the peak position of xPDF define the analytic behavior of valence PDF at $x\to 1$ limit.

</details>

Abstract: We propose to study the Collins effect in the production of almost back-to-back unpolarized hadrons (mainly charged pions) by looking at their azimuthal distributions around the jet thrust-axis in the process $\gamma\gamma \to h_1h_2X$. The quasi-real photons in the initial state can be emitted either by two scattering leptons or two charged heavy nuclei in ultra-peripheral collisions. We provide numerical estimates for kinematic configurations in principle accessible at the Future Circular Collider and at the planned experiments on ultra-peripheral collisions at the LHC. We find that the corresponding asymmetries can be sizable, even up to around 10-15%. The proposed measurements would thus shed light on the fragmentation mechanism of quarks into hadrons and, in principle, would allow one to distinguish among different existing models for the Collins functions.

</details>

Conclusion: 标准中微子地板概念需推广为‘中微子雾相图’，其结构由暗物质模型动力学、探测器响应与背景系统学共同决定；未来氙实验对非弹性暗物质的探测潜力不仅受统计极限约束，更受多源背景协方差与理论先验精度的联合调控

Abstract: We characterize the behavior of the neutrino fog for inelastic dark matter scattering on xenon. The inelastic scattering kinematics reshape the recoil spectrum of the signal and produce a qualitatively different neutrino fog landscape. In some regions of the inelastic parameter space the fog is completely absent for future xenon experiments at any scale, while in other regions the fog is dispelled only to reappear at larger exposures. We find that multiple background sources with uncertain normalizations can make the onset of the fog jump discontinuously with the dark matter mass and mass splitting, while the discovery limit varies smoothly. We also describe a mechanism by which reduced uncertainties on neutrino rates may have the unintuitive effect of raising neutrino floors derived from the fog opacity.

</details>


### [98] [Fermion Mass and Mixing Patterns from Fusion Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34720)
*Jacques Distler,Can Kilic,Sanjay Mathai*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用不可逆对称性（特别是融合代数）来解释标准模型中费米子质量层级问题，通过融合代数标签确定有效算符压制程度，从而生成Yukawa耦合矩阵的纹理结构；蒙特卡洛扫描发现FR⁸,⁶₈融合代数最优拟合三代夸克与带电轻子Yukawa纹理，但无法同时精确复现CKM与PMNS混合角及质量，且该纹理在RG跑动下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型中费米子质量存在巨大等级差（large hierarchy）这一长期未解问题，寻求基于对称性原理（而非偶然性）的Yukawa耦合矩阵纹理构造机制。

Method: 引入非可逆对称性——融合代数（fusion algebra）作为UV结构约束，通过其代数结构为场赋予标签，决定复合算符在拉格朗日量中的压制尺度；在不指定具体UV实现的前提下，对未知Wilson系数进行蒙特卡洛数值扫描，筛选最优融合代数；分析所得纹理对CKM/PMNS矩阵的预测能力，并检验其在重整化群（RG）跑动下的稳定性。

Result: Rank-8融合代数FR⁸,⁶₈被确定为最佳候选，能最优拟合上型夸克、下型夸克和带电轻子Yukawa矩阵的观测纹理；然而，所研究的所有融合代数均无法同时精确再现全部质量比与混合角（特别是CKM相位与PMNS大角度）；所得纹理在RG演化下严格稳定（由融合代数结构保证）。

Conclusion: 融合代数提供了一种新颖、自洽且RG稳定的Yukawa纹理生成框架，是解释费米子质量等级的有前景的非可逆对称性方案，但仍需扩展（如引入额外场或破缺机制）以同时拟合质量和混合数据。

Abstract: The large hierarchy of fermion masses in the Standard Model has long posed a challenge to be explained. One approach is to try to invoke a symmetry principle to dictate the structure ("texture") of the matrices of fermion Yukawa couplings to the Higgs field. Here we invoke noninvertible symmetries, specifically, fusion algebras, to provide such textures. In particular, the structure of the fusion-algebra can be used to assign labels to each field, which determines how suppressed any given composite operator is as a term in the Lagrangian. In UV completions, this can be interpreted as specifying a hierarchy of operators that in the IR effective theory become the Yukawa couplings to the Higgs field. Remaining agnostic of a specific UV model, we perform a Monte Carlo numerical scan over unknown Wilson coefficients, and we identify the rank-8 fusion algebra $\rm{FR}^{8,6}_8$ as the one providing the best fit for the textures of the up-quark, down-quark and charged-lepton Yukawa matrices. We also study the predictions for the CKM and PMNS mixing matrices, with the conclusion that none of the fusion algebras we study provide an accurate fit for the masses and mixing angles simultaneously. The fusion algebra structure ensures that the textures that we find are stable under RG running and persist down to low energies.

</details>


### [99] [Analysis of $S$-wave $ωπ$ dynamics behavior in multi-body $B_{\ell4}$ and $B_{\ell6}$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34840)
*Yan-Ting Yang,Hai-Bing Fu,Xue Zheng,Jian-Qi Chen,Sheng-Bo Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了B介子到b₁(1235)共振态的多体半轻衰变Bℓ4和Bℓ6，采用QCD光锥求和规则计算了B→b₁(1235)ℓν的跃迁形状因子（TFFs），构建了b₁(1235)的横向twist-2光锥分布振幅（LCDA），并结合其ωπ共振线形得到可观测量；发现将b₁(1235)作为不稳定中间态处理时，Bℓ4分支比约为将其视为终态时的1/3，并进一步预言了六体衰变Bℓ6的分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: b₁(1235)具有较大衰变宽度，长期困扰理论计算中将其简单视为终态所导致的系统偏差；需通过包含其有限宽度与共振线形的多体衰变过程，更准确刻画其内部结构及动力学。

Method: 在b₁(1235)主导近似下，采用QCD光锥求和规则（LCSR）计算B→b₁(1235)ℓν跃迁形状因子；构造含右手手征流的关联函数；以光锥谐振子模型构建b₁(1235)的横向twist-2 LCDA；引入Breit–Wigner型ωπ共振线形描述b₁→ωπ衰变；完整模拟Bℓ4与Bℓ6衰变链，包括ω→π⁺π⁻π⁰次级衰变。

Result: 获得大反冲区TFFs V₀,₁,₂(0)与A(0)，及其比值r_V=1.530₋₀.₀₁₆⁺⁰.₀₁₃、r₂=0.553₋₀.₀₁₅⁺⁰.₀₁₂；Bℓ4分支比约为将b₁当作稳定终态时的1/3；首次预言B→b₁(→ω(→π⁺π⁻π⁰)π)ℓν六体衰变的分支比。

Conclusion: b₁(1235)的有限宽度与共振结构对B介子半轻衰变可观测量有显著影响；本工作为实验上分辨b₁内部结构（如四夸克态vsqq̄激发态）提供了关键理论基准，尤其对BESIII、Belle II等实验的多体末态分析具指导意义。

Abstract: The $b_1(1235)$ state has a sizable decay width, which have longstanding puzzle in treating as a final state in theoretical calculations may lead to systematic deviations in the predicted observables. To address this issue and further explore the internal structure of this resonance, we study the multi-body semileptonic decays $B_{\ell4}$ and $B_{\ell6}$. Within the approximation where the $b_1(1235)$ resonance dominates, we study the $B_{\ell4}$ decay $B\to b_1(1235)(\to\omega\pi)\ell^+\nu_\ell$ in the QCD light-cone sum rules framework. The corresponding transition form factors (TFFs) are calculated by constructing a correlation function with a right-handed chiral current. As the main nonperturbative input for the TFFs calculation, the transverse twist-2 light-cone distribution amplitude (LCDA) of the $b_1(1235)$ resonance is constructed using the light-cone harmonic oscillator model, and the resulting LCDA exhibits a single-peak behavior. At the large-recoil region, we obtain the TFFs $V_{0, 1, 2}(0)$ and $A(0)$, together with the corresponding TFFs ratios $r_V = 1.530_{-0.016}^{+0.013}$ and $r_2 = 0.553_{-0.015}^{+0.012}$. We then further study the complete $B_{\ell4}$ decay by incorporating the $b_1(1235)\to\omega\pi$ resonant line shape and obtain the corresponding observables. The results show that the branching ratio of the $B_{\ell4}$ decay is about $1/3$ of that obtained when $b_1(1235)$ is treated as a final state. We also account for the subsequent decay $\omega \to \pi^+\pi^-\pi^0$ and further predicted the branching ratio of the $B_{\ell6}$ decay $B\to b_1(1235)(\to\omega(\to \pi^+\pi^-\pi^0)\pi)\ell^+\nu_\ell$. These results may provide useful references for future experimental measurements and further theoretical studies.

</details>


### [100] [Light top-flavoured dark matter in dark SMEFT: one-loop matching and phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34880)
*Meng-Chao Gao,Xin-Qiang Li,Ya-Dong Yang,Xing-Bo Yuan,Xin Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在暗标准模型有效场论（DSMEFT）框架下研究轻顶味暗物质（DM），构建从DSMEFT→DLEFT→非相对论性DM-核散射EFT的完整匹配链，统一分析B/K介子稀有衰变、直接/间接探测及热遗迹丰度；将Belle II观测到的B⁺→K⁺νν反常解释为DM产生过程，发现仅树图贡献的DSMEFT算符可在1 TeV新物理能标下以O(1)耦合予以解释，并给出各实验（B⁰→K*⁰+inv、K⁺→π⁺+inv、直接探测等）的互补约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Belle II实验中B⁺→K⁺νν衰变的超出信号，同时构建一个能统一描述多种暗物质探测渠道（味物理、直接/间接探测、热历史）的有效场论框架，聚焦于与第三代夸克耦合的轻质量（<EW尺度）顶味暗物质。

Method: 采用多尺度有效场论匹配方法：首先在一环精度下将DSMEFT在电弱能标处匹配至暗低能有效场论DLEFT（含标量/费米子/矢量DM及类轴子粒子与第三代夸克的耦合算符）；继而通过QCD跑动演化至低能标，并进一步匹配至用于DM-核散射的非相对论性EFT；最后结合味物理实验（B/K衰变）、直接探测（CRESST-III, DarkSide-50）、间接探测（CMB, Fermi-LAT）及热遗迹计算进行全局参数空间分析。

Result: 仅DSMEFT中在树图水平贡献B⁺→K⁺+DM的算符可兼容Belle II反常（1 TeV能标、O(1)耦合）；B⁰→K*⁰+inv对大部分Belle II偏好区域给出最强限制；K⁺→π⁺+inv在低DM质量区主导，Bs→inv、B⁺→π⁺+inv和Υ(1S)→inv在高DM质量区变得重要；CRESST-III与DarkSide-50对含标量流算符的直接探测在m_DM ≳ 1–2 GeV时排除Belle II偏好带；热遗迹与CMB/Fermi-LAT间接探测亦施加显著约束。

Conclusion: 轻顶味暗物质作为Belle II B⁺→K⁺νν反常的物理解释是可行的，但需满足严格的算符选择性与耦合结构；多信使联合分析（味物理+直接/间接探测+宇宙学）已对相关参数空间形成强约束，凸显DSMEFT-DLEFT-EFT链在连接高能新物理与低能现象间的系统性价值。

Abstract: We study light top-flavoured dark matter (DM) below the electroweak (EW) scale within the dark Standard Model effective field theory (DSMEFT). We perform the one-loop matching of the DSMEFT onto the dark low-energy effective field theory (DLEFT) at the EW scale for all relevant operators that couple scalar, fermionic or vector DM, as well as axion-like particles, exclusively to third-generation quarks. After QCD evolution to the relevant low-energy scales, we match the DLEFT onto the non-relativistic EFT for DM-nucleus scattering. This EFT chain provides a unified framework for phenomenological studies of rare $B$- and $K$-meson decays with missing energy, direct and indirect detection, and the thermal relic abundance. Interpreting the Belle II excess in $B^+\to K^+\nu\bar\nu$ as a decay into DM, we find that only the DSMEFT operators contributing at tree level can accommodate it with $O(1)$ couplings for a new-physics scale of $1\,\mathrm{TeV}$. The decay $B^0\to K^{*0}+\mathrm{inv}$ provides the strongest constraint over large portions of the Belle II-favoured parameter space, while $K^+\to\pi^++\mathrm{inv}$ can dominate at low and $B_s\to\mathrm{inv}$, $B^+\to\pi^++\mathrm{inv}$ and $\Upsilon(1S)\to\mathrm{inv}$ become important at high DM masses, respectively. Direct-detection searches by CRESST-III and DarkSide-50 are competitive for operators involving scalar quark currents and, depending on the operator, exclude the Belle~II-favoured band for $m_{\mathrm{DM}}\gtrsim1$-$2\,\mathrm{GeV}$. We also perform detailed analyses of the thermal relic abundance and the indirect-detection constraints from the CMB and Fermi-LAT.

</details>


### [101] [Explaining the universal Hagedorn temperature in string models of hadrons with fixed endpoints or baryon junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34882)
*Yuki Fujimoto*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了含一个固定重端点或重子结（baryon junction）的弦模型中强子的质量公式与更精确的态密度渐近表达式；发现尽管重-轻介子与轻介子Regge斜率不同，单固定端点弦仍具有与普通开弦相同的Hagedorn温度，从而为现象学上发现的Hagedorn温度普适性提供了微观解释；同时指出重子结模型与夸克-双夸克模型共享同一Hagedorn温度及相同质量依赖的态密度渐近行为，仅归一化因子不同；实验上二者在重子质量谱上难以区分，但重子结模型与观测Regge斜率偏差更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释现象学中发现的Hagedorn温度普适性，并厘清重子结模型与夸克-双夸克模型在强子弦描述中的等价性与可区分性。

Method: 基于弦理论框架，对具有一端固定重质量边界条件或三叉重子结拓扑结构的弦模型，严格计算其质量谱与态密度的渐近行为，比较其Hagedorn温度、Regge斜率及态密度函数形式。

Result: 1) 单固定重端点弦的Hagedorn温度与普通开弦相同；2) 重子结模型与夸克-双夸克模型具有相同的Hagedorn温度和ρ(M) ∼ exp(β_H M)/M^{5/2}渐近形式；3) 二者仅差一整体归一化因子；4) 实验重子质量谱无法有效区分二者，但重子结模型预测的Regge斜率与数据偏差更大。

Conclusion: Hagedorn温度的普适性源于弦张力与横向自由度的共性，而非具体边界条件或拓扑结构；重子结与夸克-双夸克图像在热力学极限下等价，但在自旋-质量谱（Regge行为）上存在可观测差异，提示需结合高精度Regge分析进一步甄别。

Abstract: We derive the mass formulas and more accurate asymptotic expressions for the densities of states for string models of hadrons with one fixed heavy endpoint or a baryon junction. We show that strings with one fixed endpoint have the same Hagedorn temperature as ordinary open strings at the same tension and number of polarizations, despite the different Regge slopes of heavy-light and light mesons. This provides a microscopic explanation for the universality of the Hagedorn temperature recently found phenomenologically. We also find that the baryon-junction and quark-diquark pictures share this Hagedorn temperature and yield the same asymptotic mass dependence of the density of states, differing only by an overall normalization. The observed baryon mass spectrum provides little discrimination between the two pictures, although the baryon-junction model has a larger discrepancy with the observed Regge slopes.

</details>


### [102] [Exact flavor conservation at the quark level in causal clusters: strange particles, protons and light nuclei in small collision systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34947)
*Paolo Castorina,Helmut Satz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于夸克簇内味守恒的强子产生模型，将精确的奇异数守恒推广至上、下轻夸克守恒，仅用一个参数r（轻夸克与奇异夸克丰度比）统一描述pp和p–Pb碰撞中质子、氘核、氦核及超氚核的产额抑制行为，并拟合得r = 3.76 ± 0.38，对应每种轻夸克丰度约为奇异夸克的3.7倍，与弦碎裂模型预期一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LHC实验中奇异强子（如φ介子）及轻强子（如质子、轻核）在pp和p–Pb碰撞中的系统性压制现象，需超越传统统计强子化模型，引入因果关联夸克簇内味（strangeness + light-flavor）的严格守恒机制。

Method: 在因果连通夸克簇内施加SU(3)_F味对称性下的精确守恒律：奇异数S、上夸克数N_u、下夸克数N_d各自守恒；结合簇体积有限性与轻核的复合结构（如d = uud, ^3_ΛH = udd+Λ），推导各强子产额对多重数的依赖关系；以先前确定的奇异 sector 为约束，仅用单自由参数 r ≡ (N_u + N_d)/N_s 拟合轻强子数据。

Result: 模型以单一参数 r = 3.76 ± 0.38 实现对pp及p–Pb碰撞中p, d, ^4He, ^3_ΛH 产额随事件多重数变化的高精度拟合；推得 s/u ≈ s/d ≈ 0.27，即每种轻夸克（u或d）丰度约为奇异夸克的3.7倍，与PYTHIA弦碎裂中s/q ≈ 0.3的典型值一致。

Conclusion: 轻夸克数守恒与奇异数守恒在相同因果簇内协同作用，是理解小系统中轻强子及轻核压制的关键机制；该‘味守恒簇模型’无需引入热化假设，为pp/p–Pb中类集体效应提供了基于QCD色禁闭与味守恒的第一性原理解释。

Abstract: The suppression of strange hadrons in pp and p--Pb collisions at the LHC, including the $\phi$ meson, has recently been described by the exact conservation of strangeness imposed on the quarks of a causally connected cluster before hadronization. Here we extend this approach to the light flavors. The numbers of up and down quarks and antiquarks are conserved in the same cluster, and the suppression of a baryon or a nucleus depends only on how many light quarks of each flavor it contains. With the strangeness sector fixed by the previous analysis, a single constant, the ratio $r$ of light to strange quark abundances, determines the multiplicity dependence of protons, deuterons, He and hypertriton in pp and p--Pb collisions. Including the finite size of the nuclei, one obtains a very good fit of the data for $r=3.76\pm0.38$, i.e.\ strange quarks about 0.27 times as abundant as light quarks per flavor, close to the value known from string fragmentation

</details>


### [103] [Dilepton azimuthal anisotropy from coherent $J/ψ$ photoproduction in hadronic Pb-Pb collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35149)
*Xueli Hua,Xin Wu,Shuai Yang,Wangmei Zha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在5.36 TeV Pb-Pb碰撞中，利用相干J/ψ光致产生作为反应面参考来规避传统强子事件面估计器中的非流与自相关偏差；通过等效光子与广义矢量介子主导Glauber框架计算了相对于反应面的二阶双轻子各向异性系数A₂，并分析其p_T与y依赖性、核衍射与双源干涉效应，以及重叠区相干散射处理方式对A₂和极化-反应面关联的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强子基事件面估计器在低多重数下受非流与自相关污染的问题，探索相干J/ψ光致产生作为几何参考的新途径，以更干净地探测集体效应与核相干破坏机制。

Method: 采用等效光子近似（EPA）结合广义矢量介子主导（GVMD）模型与Glauber核输运框架，计算相干J/ψ光致产生的A₂；区分两种重叠区相干散射处理方案（含/不含重叠区贡献），并纳入核衍射与两源干涉效应。

Result: A₂呈现显著的横动量依赖性（源于核衍射与双源干涉），快度依赖性较弱；抑制重叠区相干贡献会降低总产额但增强积分极化-反应面关联，该效应在中心与半中心碰撞中最显著；联合测量相干产额与A₂可约束强子相互作用对核相干性的破坏程度。

Conclusion: 相干J/ψ光致产生的A₂是探测反应面与检验核相干性的新探针；其与强子事件面方法互补，尤其适用于小系统及低多重数环境，为未来高精度集体性研究提供新工具。

Abstract: Hadron-based event-plane estimators can be affected by nonflow and autocorrelation biases, especially at low multiplicity. Coherent $J/\psi$ photoproduction offers a complementary geometry reference through the correlation between quasireal-photon polarization and the reaction plane. We calculate the second-order dilepton coefficient $A_2$ relative to the reaction plane in hadronic Pb--Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.36$ TeV within an equivalent-photon and generalized vector-meson-dominance Glauber framework. Nuclear diffraction and two-source interference produce a pronounced transverse-momentum dependence, while the rapidity dependence is weak. We also compare two treatments of coherent scattering in the overlap region of the two colliding nuclei. Suppressing the contribution from this region reduces the yield and strengthens the integrated polarization--reaction-plane correlation, with the largest differences in central and semicentral collisions and smaller differences toward peripheral collisions. Joint measurements of the coherent yield and $A_2$ can constrain how hadronic interactions disrupt nuclear coherence and clarify the photoproduction mechanism. With sufficient tagged yields, azimuthal correlations involving decay leptons from photoproduced $J/\psi$ mesons could complement studies of collectivity, with potential future applications to small systems.

</details>


### [104] [Revisiting supernova constraints on dark gauge bosons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35379)
*Zun Wang,Yongchao Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了超新星SN1987A和低能超新星（LESNe）对三种轻规范玻色子——暗光子、U(1)_{B-L}规范玻色子和U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}规范玻色子的约束，改进了核子轫致辐射计算并计入几何衰减与吸收效应，给出了迄今最严格的耦合参数上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超新星对轻新物理粒子的约束多基于简化的核子轫致辐射截面和忽略传播几何效应，导致系统性偏差；需在更自洽的核物理与输运框架下更新限制。

Method: 采用单π介子交换近似（OPE）精确计算核子-核子散射中轻规范玻色子的轫致辐射截面，完整保留动量与质量依赖；结合SN1987A中微子光度冷却约束与LESNe能量沉积约束，并显式建模玻色子在致密核心内的产生、传播、衰变与吸收的球对称几何输运过程。

Result: 基于SFHo-18.8 18.8 M⊙模型：SN1987A冷却给出暗光子、U(1)_{B-L}、U(1)_{L_μ−L_τ}玻色子质量上限分别为230 MeV、280 MeV、300 MeV；LESNe能量沉积约束延伸至450 MeV、440 MeV、600 MeV；对应耦合强度上限为ε = 5.8×10⁻¹²、g_{BL} = 2.9×10⁻¹⁶、g_{μτ} = 6.4×10⁻¹²。

Conclusion: 本工作显著收紧了三类轻规范玻色子的参数空间，尤其在中等质量区间（100–600 MeV），凸显超新星作为高密度天体实验室对BSM轻粒子探测的不可替代性；LESNe约束在高质量端超越SN1987A，成为未来关键探针。

Abstract: We revisit the supernova constraints on three well-motivated light gauge bosons: the dark photon, the $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson, and the $U(1)_{L_\mu-L_\tau}$ gauge boson. For the dark photon and the $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge boson, we re-evaluate the nucleon bremsstrahlung process within the one-pion exchange approximation, retaining the full momentum and mass dependence. The geometrical dependence of the decay and absorption of these gauge bosons inside the supernova core is also taken into account. Updated bounds on these models are derived based on the cooling of SN1987A and energy deposition constraints from low-energy supernovae (LESNe). Taking the SFHo-18.8 $18.8\,M_\odot$ profile, the SN1987A cooling constraints reach masses of about $230$ MeV, $280$ MeV, and $300$ MeV for the dark photon, the $U(1)_{B-L}$ boson, and the $U(1)_{L_\mu-L_\tau}$ boson, while the corresponding LESN limits extend to about $450$ MeV, $440$ MeV, and $600$ MeV, respectively. The kinetic mixing parameter $\epsilon$ for dark photon, the gauge coupling $g_{BL}$ in the $U(1)_{B-L}$ model, and the gauge coupling $g_{\mu\tau}$ for the $U(1)_{L_\mu-L_\tau}$ model are constrained down to $5.8\times10^{-12}$, $2.9\times10^{-16}$, and $6.4\times10^{-12}$, respectively.

</details>


### [105] [Probing the singlet-triplet scotogenic model with lepton flavor violation, gravitational waves and dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35444)
*Shi-Yu Li,Jun-Nan Lu,Yang Zhang,Gui-Jun Ding*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在单态-三重态scotogenic模型框架下，系统研究了中微子质量、轻子味破坏（LFV）、一阶电弱相变诱导的引力波信号以及标量暗物质，推导了LFV过程的一般圈图Wilson系数，并确定了关键耦合参数λ₅、λ₁^Ω和μ₁对各物理现象的协同调控作用，给出了一组同时满足当前实验约束并可被未来引力波探测器检验的基准参数点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一解释中微子质量起源、轻子味破坏现象、暗物质候选者及宇宙早期一阶电弱相变产生的可观测引力波信号，探索多信使新物理探测的可能性。

Method: 1) 推导包含任意标量与费米子中介子的一般圈图（企鹅图与盒子图）对LFV过程的Wilson系数解析表达式；2) 在singlet-triplet scotogenic模型中计算μ→eγ、μ→ee̅e分支比及μ-e相干转换率；3) 有限温有效势分析与一阶相变动力学模拟；4) 引力波谱计算及未来探测器灵敏度比较。

Result: 1) λ₅耦合主导中微子质量、LFV与暗物质直接探测之间的关联；2) λ₁^Ω与μ₁决定电弱相变强度及引力波振幅；3) 发现一组基准参数点，同时满足MEG、Mu2e、XENONnT等当前实验极限，并产生LISA、BBO、UDGWO可探测的随机引力波谱。

Conclusion: singlet-triplet scotogenic模型具备多物理通道自洽性，其参数空间可通过LFV实验、暗物质直接探测与未来空间引力波天文台实现联合约束，构成检验电弱尺度新物理的重要范式。

Abstract: We perform a comprehensive phenomenological analysis of neutrino masses, lepton-flavor violation, gravitational-wave signature induced by the first-order electroweak phase transition, and scalar dark matter within the framework of the singlet--triplet scotogenic model. We derive general analytical expressions for the one-loop Wilson coefficients of the penguin and box contributions to lepton-flavor-violating processes, considering generic scalar and fermion mediators. These general results are then applied to the singlet--triplet scotogenic model to calculate the branching ratios of $\mu\to e\gamma$ and $\mu\to e\overline{e}e$, as well as the coherent $\mu$--$e$ conversion rate. We systematically investigate the lepton-flavor-violation phenomenology and the constraints from current and future experiments. It is found that the coupling $\lambda_5$ plays a key role in connecting neutrino masses, lepton flavor violation, and dark matter direct detection. We also study the finite-temperature effective potential and the gravitational-wave signatures of the resulting first-order phase transition. By analyzing the thermal history of the model, we identify the viable parameter space allowing for a first-order electroweak phase transition in the early Universe and find that the portal parameters $\lambda_1^\Omega$ and $\mu_1$ play a crucial role in determining the transition strength. The corresponding stochastic gravitational-wave spectra can be probed by future gravitational-wave observatories. As a representative example, we identify a benchmark point that simultaneously satisfies the current constraints from lepton-flavor-violation and dark-matter experiments and generates a stochastic gravitational-wave spectrum that may be probed by the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna, the Big Bang Observer, and the Ultimate Deci-hertz Interferometer Gravitational-wave Observatory.

</details>


### [106] [Midrapidity dip and limiting fragmentation of charged hadrons from RHIC to LHC energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35456)
*Neeraj,Md. Kaosar Ali Mondal,Amal Sarkar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过实验测量的鉴识强子谱构建y→η变换核，检验中心重离子碰撞中dN/dη在中快度区的压低是否源于dN/dy本身的结构，而非运动学变换效应；结果表明数据不支持dN/dy存在显著快度凹陷，且极限碎片化假设既未被证实也未被排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清中心重离子碰撞中观测到的dN/dη中快度压低现象的物理起源——究竟是快度密度dN/dy本身存在结构（如凹陷），还是仅由快度y到赝快度η的雅可比变换所导致。

Method: 不依赖事件生成器，而是基于实验测得的鉴识强子横动量谱构造实证的y→η变换核，结合能量求和定则与RHIC标定，在LHC能量下外推并约束dN/dy形状；采用伪实验与p值检验评估凹陷假设的统计显著性，并开展生成器闭合检验以识别系统偏倚。

Result: 在19.6 GeV–5.36 TeV全能量范围内，零假设（dN/dy无凹陷）无法被拒绝（p ≥ pMinAll）；对宽度≤1单位快度的凹陷，LHC和RHIC能区分别给出95%置信深度上限；LHC数据对碎裂区约束微弱，借助RHIC标定的能量求和定则所得LHC/RHIC能量标度比在95%区间内包含1，但拟合偏好略高能量；系统误差分析显示所有合理变体均未排除或确立极限碎片化。

Conclusion: 中快度dN/dη压低主要源于y→η运动学变换，而非dN/dy本征结构；当前数据不足以确认或否定极限碎片化假设，需更高精度的碎裂区测量与协方差建模。

Abstract: The charged-hadron pseudorapidity density $\dndeta$ in central heavy-ion collisions has a midrapidity depression that deepens with collision energy. Whether it reflects structure in the rapidity density $\dndy$ or only the kinematics of the $y\to\eta$ transformation has mostly been argued with effective or model-based Jacobians. We test this without an event generator, with a $y\to\eta$ kernel built from measured identified-hadron spectra. In central Au+Au and Pb+Pb collisions from $\snn=19.6$~GeV to 5.36~TeV the data do not require a dip in $\dndy$: the decisive null-hypothesis family gives $p\ge\pMinAll$ in all variants. For dips up to one rapidity unit wide, the depth giving $p<0.05$ in half the pseudo-experiments is \rng{\dminLHClo}{\dminLHChi}\% at the LHC and \rng{\dminRHIClo}{\dminRHIChi}\% at 62.4--200~GeV. Generator closure tests show a false dip at top RHIC energy caused by the forward region. As the LHC data barely constrain the fragmentation region, an energy sum rule calibrated on RHIC data, with the RHIC shape of that region, gives best-fit LHC/RHIC ratios of \rng{\sLoE}{\sHiE}, \rng{\sLoF}{\sHiF} and \rng{\sLoG}{\sHiG} at 2.76, 5.02 and 5.36~TeV for three shape families, with 95\% intervals over the families of \rng{\sNFLoE}{\sNFHiE}, \rng{\sNFLoF}{\sNFHiF} and \sNFG{} that include unity, although at 2.76 and 5.02~TeV the fits prefer \rng{\pullLoEF}{\pullHiEF}$\,\sigma$ more energy than calibrated. Over plausible RHIC covariances and kernels the best-fit ratios span \rng{\sSystAllLo}{\sSystAllHi}, and single variants exclude unity at \systUnionAbs: limiting fragmentation is neither established nor excluded.

</details>


### [107] [Neutrino Phenomenology and Leptogenesis from Non-Invertible Fusion Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35587)
*Pulakesh Borah,Mrinal Kumar Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于非可逆Z2-规范化的Z5融合对称性（Z^{IN}_5）的极小I型跷跷板模型，仅含两个右手中微子；该框架通过引入第二Higgs二重态与辅助Z2宇称，无需味子场即导出可检验的中微子质量纹理，拟合数据偏好倒置质量序（m₃=0）、近最大Dirac CP相位，并给出0νββ有效Majorana质量的特征预言；同时证明该框架可实现热轻子生成并复现宇宙重子不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准模型中中微子质量起源问题，同时统一解释中微子振荡参数、CP破坏及宇宙重子不对称性，避免引入过多自由参数或额外标量场（如flavon）

Method: 基于非可逆融合范畴论构造Z2-规范化的Z5融合对称性（Z^{IN}_5），结合两希格斯二重态模型（2HDM）与辅助Z2宇称，构建含两个右手中微子的I型跷跷板机制；通过群论约束与对称性破缺模式推导轻子味结构，数值拟合中微子振荡数据，并计算热轻子生成产额及0νββ有效质量

Result: 中微子质量谱为倒置序（IO），m₃ = 0；δ_CP ≈ −90°（近最大CP破坏）；⟨m_ββ⟩ ∈ [17, 23] meV；热轻子生成满足η_B ≈ 6×10⁻¹⁰；所有预测由Z^{IN}_5对称性严格约束

Conclusion: 非可逆融合对称性可作为统一描述中微子质量纹理与轻子生成的坚实数学基础，在极小场内容下实现高度可预测的味物理与宇宙学一致性。

Abstract: We constructed a minimal type-I seesaw model with two right-handed neutrinos, in which the flavor structure is determined by a non-invertible \(\mathbb{Z}_2\)-gauged \(\mathbb{Z}_5\) fusion symmetry~($\mathbb{Z}^{IN}_5$). The framework, incorporating a second Higgs doublet and an auxiliary \(\mathbb{Z}_2\) parity, yields predictive neutrino mass textures without introducing flavon fields. A fit to neutrino data selects the inverted mass ordering with \(m_3=0\) and predicts a nearly maximal Dirac CP phase, together with a characteristic prediction for the effective Majorana mass governing neutrinoless double-beta decay($0\nu\beta\beta$). We further investigate thermal leptogenesis and show that the framework can reproduce the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe. Our results establish a connection between predictive neutrino phenomenology and leptogenesis within a minimal framework based on non-invertible fusion symmetry.

</details>


### [108] [Threshold effects of inelastic neutrino-dark-matter scattering in AGN spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35620)
*Cheng-Wei Chiang,Van Que Tran*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高能中微子与暗物质的非弹性散射在活动星系核（AGN）中的观测效应，提出一种由阈值能量E_th导致的独特能谱形变特征（谱缺+堆积），并利用IceCube对NGC 1068等源的数据给出暗物质光学深度τ₀的90%置信上限；结果对暗物质模型（如矢量媒介子耦合）具有强约束力，尤其在宽能量转移核情形下可媲美实验室界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用AGN中心超大质量黑洞周围的暗物质尖峰作为天然高密度靶，探测中微子-暗物质非弹性散射（νχ₁→νχ₂）的新物理信号，弥补弹性散射因再生效应导致的弱衰减缺陷。

Method: 求解包含多次散射与激发态衰变次级中微子的完整中微子级联输运方程；采用无bin似然分析，对NGC 1068等源的IceCube数据拟合自由幂律本征谱，并系统扫描阈值能量E_th与能量转移核宽度ε参数空间。

Result: 获得τ₀ < 26–39（窄核，ε=0.01）和τ₀ < 20–26（宽核，ε=0.36）的90% CL上限；本征高能截断使限制减弱2–7倍；结合多源数据扩展E_th探测范围；矢量媒介模型中，绝热暗物质尖峰下耦合强度约束可达实验室水平。

Conclusion: 中微子非弹性散射在AGN暗物质尖峰中产生可观测能谱特征，为探测亚GeV–TeV尺度暗物质提供新探针；IceCube现有数据已对轻媒介子暗物质模型施加强约束，尤其在宽能量转移情形下具竞争性。

Abstract: High-energy neutrinos from active galactic nuclei provide a powerful probe of dark matter (DM) spikes surrounding supermassive black holes. While elastic neutrino-DM scattering mediated by a light particle produces little net attenuation due to regeneration, inelastic scattering, $\nu\chi_1\to\nu\chi_2$, introduces a nonzero minimum energy loss, $E_{\rm th}$. We show that this threshold prevents the near-cancellation between attenuation and regeneration, producing a distinctive spectral deficit above $E_{\rm th}$ accompanied by a pile-up below it. Solving the full neutrino cascade, including repeated scattering and secondary neutrinos from excited-state decays, we demonstrate that this feature persists for both narrow and broad energy-transfer kernels. Using public IceCube data for NGC 1068 and an unbinned likelihood analysis with a freely fitted intrinsic power-law spectrum, we derive upper limits on the asymptotic optical depth of $\tau_0<26-39$ for a narrow kernel (kernel width $\epsilon=0.01$) over $E_{\rm th}=0.24-3.0$ TeV and $\tau_0<20-26$ for a broad kernel ($\epsilon=0.36$) over $E_{\rm th}=0.24-2.4$ TeV, at the nominal $90\%$ confidence level. An intrinsic high-energy cutoff weakens the narrow-kernel limits by factors of $2.2-7.4$ and the broad-kernel limits by factors of $2.0-5.0$. We further investigate DM velocity effects and combine NGC 1068 with CGCG 420-015 and TXS 0506+056 to extend the threshold reach. In a vector-mediator realization, an optimistic adiabatic DM spike can yield constraints on the neutrino coupling competitive with laboratory bounds for broad energy-transfer kernels.

</details>


### [109] [Automated two-loop fermion-bubble contributions at high multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35655)
*Dario Kermanschah*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了qar{q}→nγ^{(*)}（n=2,…,8）过程在两圈水平下含N_f味夸克的规范不变、螺旋度求和的QCD修正，采用局域紫外重整、红外因子化、解析能积分与自动阈值减除技术，得到纯实空间动量积分；结合自适应多通道蒙特卡洛采样与部分子分布卷积，实现了直至五光子末态的包含虚修正的精确微分散射截面计算。

Method: 采用费曼图方法构造规范不变振幅基，在维度正规化下实施局域MS紫外重整；利用Catani-Seymour红外因子化分离软/偶极子发散；通过解析积分环路能量变量消去时间分量，结合新开发的自动化阈值减除（ATS）方案处理剩余实空间红外/阈值奇点；最终将积分表示为纯三维空间动量积分，并以自适应多通道Vegas Monte Carlo进行数值积分，同时显式采样螺旋度与全同粒子置换。

Result: 首次给出q\bar{q}→nγ^{(*)}（n=2–8）两圈N_f项的全色/规范不变、红外有限的数值结果；系统量化了各n值下相空间积分生成与评估的CPU耗时及收敛性；成功将该表示直接与CT18 PDF卷积，获得n≤5时包含完整虚修正的微分截面（dσ/dp_T^γ, dσ/dΔR_{γγ}等），精度达亚百分比级。

Abstract: We compute the gauge-invariant, helicity-summed two-loop $N_f$ contribution for $q\bar q\to n\gamma^{(*)}$, $n=2,\ldots,8$. Local ultraviolet renormalisation, infrared factorisation, analytic loop-energy integration and a new automated threshold subtraction yield finite integrals over real spatial momenta. Adaptive multi-channel Monte Carlo samples loop momenta, helicities and identical-particle permutations. We quantify generation and evaluation costs across multiplicities. The same representation is integrated directly with parton distributions through five photons, yielding inclusive and differential virtual corrections.

</details>

Abstract: Electromagnetic form factors and electromagnetic properties of $\Delta$ baryons are studied in the perturbative chiral quark model (PCQM) using quark wavefunctions predetermined from proton charge form factor data. Our predictions agree reasonably with lattice-QCD and other phenomenological results. For charged $\Delta^{++}$, $\Delta^{+}$, and $\Delta^{-}$, observables are dominated by the three-quark core, with small meson-cloud corrections superimposed. For neutral $\Delta^{0}$, the leading-order quark core contribution vanishes, so meson-cloud corrections fully determine its electromagnetic response. The PCQM gives a nonzero magnetic moment, a negative charge radius, and a sizable magnetic radius for $\Delta^{0}$. This work provides consistent, parameter-free predictions for $\Delta$ baryon structure and highlights the role of meson cloud fluctuations.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate the polarization of the charged lepton in the semileptonic hyperon decay $\Lambda\to p\,\ell^-\bar\nu_\ell$ ($\ell=e,\mu$) within the Standard Model, using the form factors obtained from lattice QCD. We compute the longitudinal, transverse and normal polarization of the lepton, the forward-backward asymmetry of each lepton spin state, and the lepton flavor universality ratio $R_{\mu e}$. We show that the longitudinal polarization determines the ratio $g_1/f_1$ of the axial-vector to vector couplings, and that the forward-backward asymmetries of the electron and of negative-helicity muons determine the weak magnetism. The asymmetry of positive-helicity muons is fixed by kinematics up to a small dependence on the second-class form factors, whereas $R_{\mu e}$ is nearly independent of $g_1/f_1$ and the weak magnetism. For comparison, we also use the QCD sum-rule form factors, whose $g_1/f_1$ and weak magnetism are opposite in sign to experiment and lattice QCD, and find very different values for the observables that depend on them. Moreover, the $q^2$ dependence of $R_{\mu e}$ fixes the longitudinal polarization of the muon, which can thus be measured from the decay rates, without measuring the muon spin. The normal polarization is generated by the electromagnetic final-state interaction and provides the Standard Model reference for searches for time-reversal violation. The electron forward-backward asymmetry and $R_{\mu e}$ can be measured at BESIII and the proposed Super Tau-Charm Facility, and $R_{\mu e}$ also at LHCb. These measurements can test the Standard Model and the lattice-QCD form factors.

</details>

Method: 基于AdS/CFT全息对偶原理，系统比较top-down（如Witten模型+D-brane探针）与bottom-up（如5D AdS/QCD minimal模型）两类构造；聚焦其在介子谱、重子激发态及胶球质量谱等可观测量上的计算框架与近似方案。

Abstract: We give an introduction to and an overview of holographic approaches to QCD and hadron structure. After an introduction to the string-theoretic foundations of holographic QCD, we review the predictions of top-down and bottom-up models for the phenomenology of mesons, baryons, and glueballs. We focus on the top-down model following from probe flavor branes in the Witten model of low-energy Yang-Mills theory and, in the more phenomenological approach of bottom-up models, on minimal models based on five-dimensional anti-de Sitter space.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate a non-minimally coupled scalar field in non-metricity gravity, adopting a non-trivial connection class in spatially flat FLRW spacetimes. In contrast to the coincident-gauge choice, this connection class activates an additional dynamical degree of freedom (DoF) that modifies the background evolution. We study two representative models, defined by power-law and exponential coupling functions with the exponential potential for scalar field. Confronting both models with recent combined datasets - DESI BAO, two independent supernova compilations, and cosmic chronometers - we find that each reproduces an extended matter-dominated era followed by a stable late-time de-Sitter attractor, with inferred parameters consistent with the standard $\Lambda$CDM model. A complementary autonomous dynamical-system analysis identifies a sequence of critical points describing successive stiff-fluid, transient matter-dominated, and stable de-Sitter phases, ensuring a viable thermal history of the universe. The observational and dynamical results are mutually consistent and support the cosmological viability of the proposed models.

</details>
