Result: 1) 势能存在显式临界参数，当低于该值时获得唯一非退化正高度极小值，且κ²∼3b/δ；2) cuscuton推广允许κ≫1，远超原双曲势上限3/√2；3) κ≫1下，绝热高斯解给出：类dS真空、a⁻³红移的经典凝聚态、及波包中心处尘埃型零点能密度（=有效均匀谐振子零点能/物理胞腔体积）；4) 数值解证实零点能定律（误差10⁻³）、位置依赖性、Hubble率修正∝1/(cell action)，并支持κ≫1时单场‘真空+尘埃’背景等效描述；5) DV(z)与ΛCDM偏差为1.3/κ，w≡−1。

Conclusion: cuscuton型超势诱导的双曲标量势在量子宇宙学框架下，可在κ≫1区域自洽地统一描述暗能量（w=−1真空）与暗物质（量子零点能驱动的尘埃行为），无需经验拟合；其可观测偏差（如DV）由单一参数κ控制，为检验量子引力起源的暗宇宙提供了可证伪的低能唯象窗口。

Abstract: We quantize a spatially flat FLRW minisuperspace containing one canonical scalar field with the hyperbolic potential generated by a cuscuton-type superpotential, and extract cosmological histories from the Wheeler-DeWitt wave function in the de Broglie-Bohm interpretation. For the canonical potential parameter, related to the original cuscuton couplings, below an explicit critical value the potential has a unique nondegenerate minimum with positive height, and the ratio $\kappa$ of the scalar mass to the de Sitter Hubble rate at the minimum diverges as $\kappa^2\simeq 3b/\delta$, where $\delta$ is the relative distance to criticality. For the original hyperbolic potential, $\kappa\leq 3/\sqrt{2}$, whereas its cuscuton-inspired generalization allows a large hierarchy between scalar oscillation and expansion rates. For $\kappa\gg1$, small excitation and sufficiently large cell action, the reduced wave equation has an adiabatic Gaussian solution describing a de Sitter vacuum, a classical oscillating condensate whose averaged excitation density redshifts as $a^{-3}$, and, at the packet centre, a leading quantum density equal to the zero-point energy of the effective homogeneous oscillator divided by the physical cell volume, which is also dust-like. Full numerical solutions of the Wheeler-DeWitt equation support the averaged zero-point law at the reported $10^{-3}$ level, reproduce its dependence on the position of the Bohmian particle inside the packet, show that quantum corrections to the present Hubble rate converge as the inverse of the cell action, and support a single-field dust-plus-vacuum description at the homogeneous background level when $\kappa\gg1$. The classical histories approach the $\Lambda$CDM distance relations with a deviation of $1.3/\kappa$ in $D_V$. The dark energy component has $w=-1$ by construction, and the model is not fitted to data.

</details>


### [115] [Black holes from a nonlocal equation of state: horizon geometry, singularity structure and thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33222)
*Hishan Farfán-Bachiloglu,C. R. Muniz,Francisco Tello-Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在静态球对称Kerr–Schild度规下，各向异性流体满足非局域状态方程时的精确解；导出Yukawa型密度分布，揭示有限程‘毛发’结构，并系统分类其黑洞/类黑洞几何（含双视界、极值与无视界构型）；证明内视界表面引力恒大于外视界；分析奇点强度（Królak强曲率、Tipler强奇点），讨论曲率标量的可积性；推广至幂律非局域闭合，获得含不完全Γ函数的解族，实现从奇点到正则de Sitter核的插值；推导闭式表面引力、拓展第一定律与Smarr关系，并利用Lambert W函数下支定位热容发散点；最后以热力学拓扑区分单缺陷Schwarzschild类相与双反向缠绕缺陷的极值相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有物理合理非局域性（如量子引力或有效场论启发）的引力模型，规避经典奇点并赋予黑洞有限程微观结构（'hair'），同时保持解析可解性与热力学一致性。

Method: 采用静态球对称Kerr–Schild度规框架，引入各向异性流体的非局域物态方程，求解爱因斯坦方程；运用渐近分析、奇点强度判据（Królak/Tipler）、曲率标量可积性检验、热力学微分几何（表面引力、第一定律、Smarr关系、热容与Lambert函数分析）及拓扑缺陷分类方法。

Result: 1) 唯一导出Yukawa型质量密度；2) 获得四类精确几何：无视界、Schwarzschild型单视界、双视界（含Cauchy视界）与极值解（质量与半径闭式表达）；3) 严格证明双视界区κ⁻ > κ⁺；4) 零中心质量极限对应全局单极子（静态支）或Kantowski–Sachs区域（非静态支）；5) 奇点满足Królak强曲率条件且为Tipler强奇点（非径向测地线）；6) R与√K在原点处对不变体积局部可积；7) 幂律推广解由不完全Γ函数表出，实现奇点→正则de Sitter核连续过渡；8) 得到闭式表面引力、拓展第一定律与Smarr关系；9) 热容发散点由Lambert W₋₁函数显式定位；10) 热力学相图呈现单缺陷（Schwarzschild类）与双反向缺陷（极值类）的拓扑区分。

Conclusion: 非局域物态方程可自然产生具有有限程结构、可控奇点性质与丰富热力学相结构的Kerr–Schild黑洞解；该框架无需非线性电动力学即可实现正则化，并为量子引力中‘软化’奇点与编码微观自由度提供新范式。

Abstract: We show that a nonlocal equation of state for an anisotropic fluid reduces to a linear equation for the enclosed matter and uniquely selects a Yukawa-type density profile in a static, spherically symmetric Kerr--Schild geometry. The resulting neutral solution has finite-range hair, exponentially screened at large distances, with the asymptotic mass as its only conserved charge, and admits an exact horizon classification: horizonless configurations, Schwarzschild-like black holes, two-horizon geometries with a Cauchy horizon, and an extremal configuration whose mass and radius are obtained in closed form. We prove that the magnitude of the inner surface gravity exceeds that of the outer horizon throughout the two-horizon regime. When the central mass vanishes, the geometry approaches a global monopole in the static branch and a Kantowski--Sachs-type region in the nonstatic branch. The singularity satisfies Królak's strong-curvature criterion and is Tipler-strong along non-radial geodesics in the spacelike branch. For vanishing central mass, the Ricci scalar and the square root of the Kretschmann scalar are locally integrable with respect to the invariant spacetime volume, despite diverging at the origin. A power-law generalization of the nonlocal closure yields solutions expressed through incomplete gamma functions, interpolating between the singular core and a regular black hole with a de~Sitter core, without invoking nonlinear electrodynamics. We obtain closed-form surface gravities, an extended first-law identity and a Smarr relation, and locate the heat-capacity divergence using the lower branch of the Lambert function. Finally, thermodynamic topology distinguishes a Schwarzschild-like regime with one defect from an extremal regime with two opposite-winding defects that merge at the maximum Hawking temperature.

</details>


### [116] [Hyperboloidal Initial Data with Prescribed Asymptotic Charges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33266)
*Károly Csukás,János Östör,István Rácz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新方法，通过Fuchsian分析启发的谱求解器与抛物-双曲型约束方程结合，在求解爱因斯坦约束方程前直接解析控制渐近荷（如Bondi能量与角动量），并以自旋黑洞为例构造了具指定Bondi能量EB=2和角动量JB=1/2的双曲面初值数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双曲面初值构造难以在解约束前精确指定物理上可观测的渐近守恒量（如Bondi能量、角动量）；需发展能直接参数化渐近自由度、确保渐近平坦性与物理可解释性的新框架。

Method: 采用抛物-双曲型爱因斯坦约束方程表述，结合Fuchsian分析思想设计谱数值求解器，将渐近自由度（对应无穷阶渐近荷塔）作为边界条件显式输入，从而实现对Bondi能量、角动量等渐近量的解析控制。

Result: 成功构造出满足指定Bondi能量EB=2与角动量JB=1/2的旋转黑洞双曲面初值数据，起始种子数据取自质量M=1的Schwarzschild时空的双曲切片，验证了该方法的可行性与精度。

Conclusion: 该方法建立了渐近荷与双曲面初值之间的显式映射，为双曲面演化、引力波提取及渐近对称性研究提供了可控、解析兼容的初值生成范式。

Abstract: A novel method is introduced for constructing hyperboloidal initial data with direct analytic control over asymptotic charges prior to solving the Einstein constraint equations. Using the parabolic-hyperbolic formulation of the constraints together with a Fuchsian-analysis-inspired spectral solver, the asymptotic degrees of freedom, which determine infinite towers of asymptotic charges, including the Bondi energy and angular momentum, are used to prescribe the asymptotic behavior of solutions. As a proof of concept, we explicitly construct hyperboloidal initial data for a spinning black hole with Bondi energy $E_B = 2$ and angular momentum $J_B = 1/2$, starting from seed data induced on a hyperboloidal slice of the non-rotating Schwarzschild spacetime with mass $M=1$.

</details>


### [117] [Impact of nonlinear electrodynamics on quasinormal modes of rotating black strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33309)
*F. S. Mirekhtiary,I. Sakalli*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了由带负指数s的幂律Maxwell场源驱动的旋转反德西特（AdS）黑弦上的无质量标量扰动谱，发现电荷与转动共同导致丰富的准正规模结构：包括例外点、非振荡衰减、超几何谱端点、动量依赖的光锥偏离行为，以及无超辐射的纯阻尼轴对称模塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维AdS黑洞/黑弦在非线性电磁场下的稳定性及动力学行为，特别是电荷与转动耦合对准正规模谱的影响，检验近视界近似方法的适用边界。

Method: 采用Horowitz-Hubeny级数展开、Chebyshev配置法和时域演化三种互补数值方法，并各自实现两套独立代码；所有结果均与平面Schwarzschild-AdS极限交叉验证。

Result: 发现电荷参数\hat q_c ≈ 0.72626处存在例外点（exceptional point），此时最低阻尼模对在虚轴上合并，导致强荷黑弦无振荡衰减；谱端点精确为超几何函数；大横向动量k下模频偏离光锥标度为k^{1/(1−4s)}；转动将例外点延拓为(q, a)平面上的一条线，激发纯阻尼轴对称模塔，且不引发超辐射不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该几何中准正规模谱受电荷与转动协同调控，呈现丰富临界现象；先前基于近视界近似的分析遗漏了所有这些关键特征，表明全局结构对稳定性分析至关重要。

Abstract: We study massless scalar perturbations of rotating anti-de Sitter black strings sourced by a power-Maxwell field with negative exponent $s$. The charge enters the metric function as $-Qr^{p}$ with $p=2/(1-2s)$, and the case $s=-1/2$ gives a linear term with an algebraic horizon. Since rotation is a local boost of the static string, the rotating spectrum follows exactly from the static one evaluated at a frequency-dependent momentum. We compute the spectrum with the Horowitz-Hubeny series, Chebyshev collocation and time-domain evolution, each implemented in two independent codes and checked against the planar Schwarzschild-AdS limit. The charge acts in two ways. At the reduced charge $\hat q_c=0.72626$ the least damped pair of modes meets on the imaginary axis, so more strongly charged strings ring down without oscillation, and at the endpoint of the branch the spectrum is exactly hypergeometric. At large transverse momentum the distance of the modes from the light cone grows as $k^{1/(1-4s)}$, replacing the $k^{-1/5}$ law of the neutral brane. Rotation continues the exceptional point into a line in the plane of charge and rotation, adds a purely damped axisymmetric tower, and never produces superradiant growth. A near-horizon estimate used earlier for this geometry misses all of these effects.

</details>


### [118] [Neutron Star--Primordial Black Hole Binaries: Dynamical Tides, Resonance Locking, and Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33314)
*Isaac Legred,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究中子星与原初黑洞组成的双星系统的轨道演化，指出动力学潮汐效应（类比太阳系巨行星-卫星系统）可加速并合或导致外旋，并通过引力波辐射实现共振锁定，可能在脉冲星计时和引力波信号中留下可观测特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中子星与原初黑洞这类质量悬殊紧凑双星系统的非标准轨道演化机制，尤其是传统2.5阶后牛顿近似失效情形下动力学潮汐与引力波辐射的耦合效应。

Method: 基于共振潮汐锁定假设，结合恒星振荡模式（如f模、p模）与轨道频率的共振条件，构建包含潮汐耗散与引力波反作用的动力学轨道演化方程。

Result: 发现当中子星本征振动模式与轨道频率保持共振时，引力波辐射可显著增强或抑制潮汐能量交换，导致轨道衰减率出现非单调依赖于偏心率与自转倾角的特征；特定参数下可发生稳定外旋。

Conclusion: 该机制为探测亚太阳质量原初黑洞提供了新探针——其轨道演化异常可能在毫秒脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）或未来空间引力波天文台（如LISA）中表现为相位异常或共振频率调制。

Abstract: The orbital evolution of a neutron star and a much lower-mass primordial black hole can differ strikingly from that of typical compact binaries. Dynamical tides, analogous to those in the Solar System's giant planet-moon systems, can accelerate inspiral or induce outspiral, depending on the orbital configuration. We show how gravitational-wave emission mediates this evolution when neutron star modes remain locked in resonance with the orbit. These effects may leave observable signatures in pulsar timing or gravitational waves.

</details>


### [119] [Constraining Ultralight Dark Matter in Dynamical Dark Energy Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33332)
*Muhammad Awais,Hafiz Inam Ullah,Rafael C. Nunes,Paula S. Ferreira,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量场暗物质（SFDM）在动力学暗能量框架下的宇宙学影响，结合CMB、BAO和SNe数据进行贝叶斯参数估计，得到SFDM质量下限 log₁₀(m_ϕ/eV) > −24.04，并发现SFDM不改变动力学暗能量的演化证据（含低红移幽灵穿越）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展此前仅在ΛCDM框架下研究SFDM的工作，探究SFDM与动力学暗能量共存时对背景演化和线性扰动的影响，检验其是否改变暗能量演化特征。

Method: 在背景及线性微扰层次自洽构建SFDM + 动力学暗能量模型；采用Planck CMB、DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+ SNe Ia数据进行贝叶斯参数推断；分析质量参数对P(k)和CMB TT谱的尺度依赖调制效应。

Result: 获得SFDM标量质量95%单边下限：log₁₀(m_ϕ/eV) > −24.04；轻质量导致显著的尺度依赖物质功率抑制和CMB TT谱修正，重质量趋近CDM极限；SFDM替换CDM不削弱动力学暗能量演化（含w < −1的低z幽灵穿越）的统计证据。

Conclusion: SFDM与动力学暗能量可相容，其存在不破坏当前对暗能量状态方程演化的观测支持；质量约束强化了超轻轴子类SFDM（m_ϕ ∼ 10⁻²⁴ eV）仍具可行性，但更轻质量被排除。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological implications of scalar-field dark matter (SFDM) in a dynamical dark energy framework, extending previous analyses that considered SFDM within the standard $\Lambda$CDM cosmological model. We consistently implement the scenario at both the background and linear perturbation levels. We perform Bayesian parameter inference using Planck CMB measurements, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data, and Pantheon+ Type Ia supernovae to constrain the model parameter space. Our theoretical predictions show that lighter scalar-field masses produce increasingly strong scale-dependent suppression of matter fluctuations and more pronounced modifications to the CMB temperature power spectrum, whereas heavier masses progressively approach the cold dark matter limit. For the SFDM mass, we obtain a one-sided 95\% lower limit of $\log_{10}(m_\phi/{\rm eV})>-24.04$ from our main joint analysis. We find that replacing the standard cold dark matter fluid with SFDM does not qualitatively alter the constraints on dynamical dark energy or the evidence for its evolution, including a phantom crossing at low redshifts.

</details>


### [120] [Accurate pulsar timing array likelihoods: better low-rank covariance for gravitational wave background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33444)
*Heling Deng,Ken D. Olum,Rutger van Haasteren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的低秩协方差建模方法，用于脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）中引力波背景（GWB）的谱建模，克服了传统傅里叶对角模型在亚0.1 yr⁻¹尺度上无法分辨谱特征导致参数推断偏差的问题。新方法通过在真实模型似然系综上最大化平均对数似然构建非对角基域协方差，显著提升谱特征恢复保真度与似然精度，且计算代价与传统方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PTA分析中采用离散傅里叶基（频率间隔~0.1 yr⁻¹）加对角协方差建模GWB，虽高效但因频域分辨率不足而忽略亚0.1 yr⁻¹尺度的谱结构，导致似然失真与参数推断偏差。

Method: 提出基于系综平均对数似然最大化的低秩协方差构造法：在选定基（如傅里叶或自适应基）下，优化基域协方差矩阵（一般为非对角），使其在真实GWB功率谱密度（PSD）生成的信号实现系综上最大化平均似然；该协方差显式依赖于全谱形状，而非仅采样点处的PSD值。

Result: 在两个mock数据集上验证：（1）似然函数更接近真实后验；（2）对弯曲谱、截断谱等精细谱特征的恢复显著优于传统对角傅里叶模型；（3）计算开销与标准傅里叶对角方法相当。

Conclusion: 该非对角低秩协方差建模范式可作为PTA GWB分析的新标准，在不牺牲计算效率的前提下，消除由频域离散化引入的系统性谱失真，提升对宇宙学和天体物理GWB源的参数约束可靠性。

Abstract: In pulsar timing array analyses, the stochastic process describing the gravitational wave background is typically modeled using a Fourier basis at discrete frequencies with an interval $\sim 0.1~\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, with a diagonal Fourier-domain covariance determined by the power spectral density at the corresponding frequencies. Although this rank-reduced modeling significantly accelerates likelihood evaluation and provides a good approximation in many scenarios, it can lead to biased parameter inference because it is insensitive to spectral features on scales smaller than $\sim 0.1~\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. We introduce a better construction of the low-rank covariance for low-frequency processes by maximizing the log-likelihood averaged over an ensemble of realizations drawn from the true model likelihood. In this approach, the basis-domain covariance matrix is sensitive to the entire model spectrum and is generally non-diagonal. Using two mock datasets, we demonstrate improved likelihood accuracy and more faithful recovery of spectral features compared to the conventional Fourier-diagonal model. Benchmark tests show that this new model requires a computational cost comparable to that of the conventional approach.

</details>


### [121] [Acoustic Schwarzschild-like black hole and analogous Hawking radiation in Einstein-bumblebee gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33481)
*Chikun Ding,Yuebing Zhou,Yu Shi,Yuehua Xiao,Jiliang Jing*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Einstein-bumblebee理论框架下，借助Gross-Pitaevskii理论构造了一个声学类Schwarzschild黑洞，并研究其类比Hawking辐射；该模型具有引力视界与内/外声学视界，温度与灰体因子表现出对Lorentz破缺参数ℓ和调谐参数ξ的特征依赖关系，其中声学霍金温度存在上限T_acm = T_h/8，且灰体因子与辐射谱对ξ、ℓ呈相反依赖，为实验检验Lorentz破缺提供了可观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Lorentz对称性破缺（通过bumblebee场实现）对类比引力系统中霍金辐射的影响，建立可被冷原子实验探测的声学黑洞模型。

Method: 基于Einstein-bumblebee引力理论，在Schwarzschild-like度规背景下应用Gross-Pitaevskii方程描述超流体背景，推导有效声学度规并识别声学视界；进而采用Bogoliubov变换计算标量粒子的类比Hawking辐射谱、灰体因子及能谱发射率。

Result: 1) 出现三重视界结构：r_h=2M（引力视界）、r_ac−∈(2M,4M]与r_ac≥4M（内/外声学视界），由ℓ与ξ共同决定；2) 声学霍金温度T_ac存在最大值T_acm=T_h/8；3) 低频灰体因子Γ(ω)随ξ增大而增大、随ℓ增大而减小；高频极限Γ→1；4) 能谱dE/dω呈现类黑体峰形，峰值频率ω_m处辐射最强，且dE/dω整体随ξ增大而增强、随ℓ增大而抑制。

Conclusion: Lorentz破缺参数ℓ与调谐参数ξ在声学黑洞辐射中引入可区分的、反向的物理效应，尤其灰体因子与能谱对二者相反的依赖关系构成关键可观测量，为未来冷原子模拟实验或天体观测中检验Einstein-bumblebee理论提供了明确判据。

Abstract: We derive an acoustic Schwarzschild-like black hole and study its analogous Hawking radiation in Einstein-bumblebee theory by using Gross-Pitaevskii theory in the background of Schwarzschild-like spacetime. This black hole have two kinds of event horizons: one is the gravitational horizon $r_h=2M$(black hole horizon), the others are the internal and external acoustic horizons $2M<r_{ac-}\leq4M,\;r_{ac}\geq4M$(dumb hole horizon) which depend on the tuning parameter $\xi$ and Lorentz violation coupling constant $\ell$. The corresponding Hawking temperature $T_h$ depends only on $\ell$, however $T_{ac}$ depends on $\ell$ and $\xi$. The dumb hole temperature $T_{ac}$ has a maximum $T_{acm}=T_h/8$. When considering the scalar particles radiating from the dumb hole, we find that in low frequency, the grey-body factor increases with the tuning parameter $\xi$, but decreases with $\ell$. In the high frequency, it approaches to $1$. Its energy emission rate is similar to the Planck's black spectrum where there has a maximum at a given peak frequency $\omega_m$. The energy emission rate also increases with the tuning parameter $\xi$, but decreases with $\ell$. This opposite dependence relationship is the most experimentally valuable feature and can directly benchmark against observations of real astrophysical black holes.

</details>


### [122] [Gravitational Waves from Ringing Dyonic Black Holes and Estimation of Parameters in Einstein-Bumblebee-Maxwell Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33569)
*Sikha Gogoi,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Einstein-bumblebee-Maxwell引力理论中类荷电Reissner-Nordström黑洞的标量与张量型准正规模（QNM），采用6阶Padé平均WKB方法计算，并通过时域演化验证；进一步利用Fisher分析评估LISA对超大质量黑洞质量及洛伦兹破缺参数的测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验洛伦兹对称性破缺（由bumblebee场诱导）对黑洞动力学稳定性及引力波环下信号的影响，探索其在空间引力波探测中的可观测性。

Method: 采用6阶Padé平均WKB近似计算准正规模；辅以时域数值积分提取主导QNM；结合Fisher信息矩阵分析LISA对黑洞质量M与bumblebee洛伦兹破缺参数ℓ的参数估计精度。

Result: 发现超大质量黑洞（10⁶–10⁸ M⊙）在低红移下LISA信噪比显著；对3 Gpc处源，M ≥ 10⁶ M⊙时，M与ℓ可被良好约束；QNM实部（振荡频率）与虚部（衰减率）均显式依赖于洛伦兹破缺参数。

Conclusion: Einstein-bumblebee-Maxwell框架下的RN-like黑洞QNM谱具有可观测的洛伦兹破缺特征，LISA有望通过环下信号实现对新物理参数ℓ的直接限制，为量子引力唯象学提供新探针。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the quasinormal mode (QNM) frequencies of dyonic Reissner-Nordström (RN)-like black holes associated with scalar and gravitational perturbations in the framework of Einstein-bumblebee-Maxwell gravity theory. The QNMs are calculated using the 6th-order Padé averaged WKB approximation method. We analyse the dependence of oscillation frequencies and damping rate of the BHs for the free model parameters. To further verify the QNM spectrum, we study the time evolution of both the scalar and gravitational perturbations around the BHs and extract the dominant QNM from the resulting ringdown signal. Moreover, we try to estimate the BH parameters using Fisher analysis method for the future gravitational wave detector named Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). We found that for supermassive BHs with mass around $10^6$ -- $10^8 M_\odot$ and at lower redshifts, the signal-to-noise ratio for the detection of a QNM signal by the LISA is high. Further, for a representative source at a luminosity distance of $3$ Gpc, the BH mass at the detector reference frame and the Lorentz violating parameter are well constrained for supermassive BHs with mass $\ge 10^6.$

</details>


### [123] [A no-go theorem for closed timelike curves in globally null-foliated spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33706)
*Hongsheng Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种判定闭合类时曲线（CTC）不存在的新判据：若时空存在全局定义的零梯度函数（即满足零程函方程），则必无CTC；该判据独立于稳定因果性，且仅依赖一个标量方程，不涉及能量条件。作者将此应用于Kundt与Robinson–Trautman时空，证明其在零坐标单值区域中不可能含CTC，并指出Misner、Ori与Ahmed构造的本质是紧致零方向转为类时方向，而宇宙学常数、膨胀与剪切均为旁观量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CTC排除判据分为三类（时间函数、能量条件、拓扑），均存在适用限制；作者旨在构建更基本、更普适、不依赖能量条件的新判据。

Method: 利用零协向量与类时向量正交性为零这一代数事实，证明满足全局零程函方程（∇_a f ∇^a f = 0）的函数f沿任意类时曲线严格单调，从而排除CTC；进而将该判据应用于Kundt、Robinson–Trautman及Misner/Ori/Ahmed类时空的几何结构分析。

Result: 1) 首次证明：任意Kundt与Robinson–Trautman时空，在零坐标全局单值的区域上，无论Λ与旋转1-形式如何，均不含CTC；2) 揭示Misner、Ori、Ahmed构造的统一几何本质——紧致零方向发生类时化；3) 证明该零程函判据逻辑上独立于稳定因果性，且比经典时间函数定理更具适用性。

Conclusion: 零程函判据构成CTC排除的第四类标准，其基础性、代数简洁性与几何普适性拓展了因果结构分析的工具集，尤其适用于能量条件失效或难以验证的情形。

Abstract: The study of closed timelike curves has proceeded one spacetime at a time, and the criteria available form three families, the time function criteria, the energy condition criteria, and the topological criteria. We initiate a fourth family of criteria, elementary in form and resting on a single scalar equation rather than any energy condition. We prove that a spacetime admitting a globally defined function whose gradient is everywhere null cannot contain a closed timelike curve, and that the hypothesis is logically independent of stable causality rather than weaker than it. The proof turns on one algebraic fact, that a null covector is never orthogonal to a timelike vector, so the function grows strictly along every timelike curve and cannot close. Because the null eikonal equation alone decides the criterion, we can use it where the classical time function theorem is silent. We establish for the first time that no Kundt spacetime and no Robinson--Trautman spacetime can contain a closed timelike curve on any region where the null coordinate is globally single valued, whatever the cosmological constant and the rotation one form, which settles a question left open for gyratonic waves. We further show that the Misner, Ori and Ahmed constructions reduce to the statement that a compact null direction has turned timelike, and that the cosmological constant, the expansion and the shear are spectators in all of them.

</details>


### [124] [Forecasting Strongly Lensed Binary Black Hole Rates from the Stochastic Gravitational-Wave Background with TianQin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33751)
*Zheng-Cheng Liang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用天琴（TianQin）空间引力波探测器在毫赫兹频段对随机引力波背景（SGWB）的观测，来约束恒星质量双黑洞（BBH）整体种群参数，并进一步预测地面引力波探测器可观测的强引力透镜BBH并合事件率的新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立毫赫兹SGWB与地面探测器可观测的强透镜BBH事件之间的定量联系，提升对BBH宇宙并合历史和透镜统计的联合约束能力。

Method: 构建基于TianQin单探测器一年模拟数据的SGWB反演框架，联合BBH种群模型与星系尺度透镜模型（含图像级选择效应），区分‘至少一个像被探测’的透镜事件率 R_event^det 与‘两个像均被探测且成功关联’的透镜系统率 R_sys^det，并计算可探测系统分数 F_sys^det。

Result: 在系统总放大率阈值 μ_sys ≥ 2 下，中位预测值为：R_event^det = 5.50×10⁻⁴ yr⁻¹，R_sys^det = 2.54×10⁻⁴ yr⁻¹，F_sys^det = 3.35×10⁻⁴；相比仅用先验的传播，后验传播使各量16–84百分位宽度分别减小6.5%、6.4%和4.0%。

Conclusion: TianQin对毫赫兹SGWB的测量可显著收紧强透镜BBH事件的预测不确定性，为多信使引力波天文学提供关键种群-透镜协同分析范式。

Abstract: The millihertz stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) provides a population-level probe of stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) and their cosmic merger history. We develop a framework using TianQin alone to propagate SGWB-informed BBH population constraints into forecasts of strongly lensed BBH mergers detectable by ground-based gravitational-wave detectors. The framework distinguishes between lensed events with at least one detected image and lens systems in which both images are detected and successfully associated. Using a one-year mock observation, we infer the BBH population and propagate the resulting posterior through a galaxy-scale lensing model with image-level selection. For the adopted system-magnification threshold, \(\mu_{\rm sys}\ge 2\), the median predicted rate of lensed events with at least one detected image is \(R_{\rm event}^{\rm det}=5.50\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}\), while the median rate of associated two-image lens systems is \(R_{\rm sys}^{\rm det}=2.54\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}\); the median detectable system fraction is \(\mathcal{F}_{\rm sys}^{\rm det}=3.35\times10^{-4}\). Compared with the prior-only propagation, the pooled 16th--84th percentile widths decrease by 6.5\% for the event rate, 6.4\% for the system rate, and 4.0\% for the system fraction. Our work establishes a quantitative link between millihertz SGWB inference and forecasts of strongly lensed BBH mergers for ground-based gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [125] [Massive Scalar Quasinormal Modes and Quasibound States of Charged Black Holes with Logarithmic corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33883)
*Prosenjit Paul,Rickmoy Samanta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了对数形变的Reissner-Nordström黑洞度规中，中性标量场的准正规模（QNM）与准束缚态（QBS）行为；发现标量质量与非线性耦合增强束缚但缩短寿命，并通过多种数值/解析方法验证了结果一致性，最后在相对论双线性框架下证实QNM-QBS正交性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解长程度规变形如何影响黑洞附近标量场的动力学，特别是振荡、束缚与衰减行为；该几何既来自非线性电动力学的精确解，也对应于完美流体暗物质唯象模型，具有理论与观测双重动机。

Method: 采用对数形变的RN度规（由非线性电动力学导出），结合一阶与六阶WKB近似、全局匹配法、推广的Leaver方法（适配对数奇点）及时域演化；引入适当视界正则化与复径向路径，在相对论双线性框架下检验QNM-QBS正交性。

Result: 小标量质量使有效势垒外移、升高且曲率减小，导致各有限多极与泛音的阻尼减弱；QNM与QBS计算在不同方法间一致；Padé近似对束缚能误差约0.04%，Gamow隧穿低估阻尼约25%；增大标量质量或非线性耦合增强束缚但因视界吸收增强而缩短寿命；首次在该类对数几何中严格验证QNM-QBS正交性。

Conclusion: 长程度规变形显著调控标量场的谱结构：束缚与衰减存在本质权衡；多种互补方法可高精度刻画QNM/QBS；相对论双线性正交性在含对数奇点的渐近平直黑洞中仍成立，为模展开与响应理论提供坚实基础。

Abstract: We study how long-range deformations in a black-hole metric affect the oscillation, trapping, and decay of a neutral, minimally coupled massive scalar field. We choose a logarithmic deformation of Reissner-Nordström (RN) sourced by nonlinear electrodynamics. It provides an exact solution of Einstein gravity and admits a finite completion. The same geometry also arises in a phenomenological perfect-fluid dark-matter model. In this setup, we analyze both quasinormal modes (QNMs), which describe relaxation, and quasibound states (QBSs), which describe trapped modes with small horizon absorption. We first show that a small scalar mass moves the potential barrier outward, raises it, and reduces its curvature. First-order WKB then predicts weaker damping at every fixed finite multipole and overtone. We then compare several approaches, namely sixth-order WKB, global matching, a generalized Leaver method (adapted to the logarithmic geometry) and time-domain evolution. All methods yield consistent QNM estimates within their ranges of validity. Global matching and Leaver also agree closely for QBSs. Further analytic estimates with a reference trapped state show that a local Padé approximation gives a binding-energy error of about $0.04\%$, while leading Gamow tunnelling underestimates damping by about $25\%$. Across the sampled QBSs, increasing the scalar mass or nonlinear coupling strengthens binding but shortens the lifetime through greater horizon absorption. Finally, using an appropriate horizon regularization and a common complex radial contour, we demonstrate orthogonality of an independently computed QNM-QBS pair using a suitable relativistic bilinear framework.

</details>


### [126] [Gravitational Radiation Coupled to a real massive scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33928)
*Yue Ma,Xianghao Wu,Weidong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了正质量实标量场下Einstein--Klein--Gordon系统在波规范下的小初值全局解的渐近行为，证明了度规扰动的线性化迹分量具有最优衰减率 $\hat t^{-1} \simeq t^{-1}$，并建立了初始Klein-Gordon能量由该主导系数定量控制的一点可观测性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论与量子场论耦合系统（如Einstein--Klein--Gordon）在非线性、渐近平坦时空中的长期动力学行为，特别是度规与物质场之间的能量传递与衰减结构。

Method: 在波规范（wave gauge）下进行高阶能量估计与渐近分析，结合Klein-Gordon方程的色散性质与Einstein方程的双曲结构，提取度规扰动的线性化迹分量，并利用辐射场构造与精细的$\hat t$-加权估计建立可观测性不等式。

Result: 1. 度规扰动的线性化迹分量以 $t^{-1}$ 速率最优衰减；2. 初始Klein-Gordon能量可被该迹分量的前导系数定量控制（即一点可观测性估计成立）。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了Einstein--Klein--Gordon系统中引力与标量场耦合所诱导的特定衰减层级与能量可观测性结构，为非线性稳定性与散射理论提供了关键先验控制。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of small-data global solutions to the Einstein--Klein--Gordon system with a positive-mass real scalar field in wave gauge. We show that the linearized trace component of the metric perturbation admits a $\hat t^{-1}\simeq t^{-1}$ optimal decreasing rate. Moreover, a quantitative one-point observability estimate is established: the initial Klein-Gordon energy is controlled by this leading order coefficient.

</details>


### [127] [Comment on "Effect of perfect fluid dark matter..." [Phys. Dark Univ. 34 (2021) 100891]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34019)
*Ming-Hsun Li,Kwei-Chou Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文澄清了Shaymatov等人所用黑洞度规的物理诠释，指出该度规实际描述冷暗物质与幻影场构成的双组分系统，而非单组分完美流体暗物质（PFDM）；强调以总状态方程 $w_{\mathrm{total}} = -1$ 视为PFDM会导致引力吸引失效，违背暗物质晕形成要求，并厘清原模型中不同参数区间的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正对Li-Yang (2012) 度规的误用，防止将其错误诠释为单组分完美流体暗物质（PFDM），尤其避免在极端天体物理场景中因参数混淆导致的物理不自洽。

Method: 基于广义相对论中的能量-动量张量分解与状态方程分析，结合度规源项的物理可实现性判据（如弱能量条件、引力聚合作用），辨析双组分（冷暗物质+幻影场）与单组分PFDM在几何与动力学上的本质区别。

Result: 确认该度规对应双组分系统：冷暗物质（$w=0$）提供引力束缚，幻影场（$w<-1$）主导宇宙学演化；其总有效状态方程 $w_{\mathrm{total}} = -1$ 不支持静态暗物质晕结构，故不能等效为PFDM。

Conclusion: 将该度规诠释为PFDM是概念性错误；正确物理解释必须区分组分贡献，且参数选取须严格限定于原模型定义的物理相容区域。

Abstract: This Comment clarifies the physical interpretation of the black hole spacetime metric employed by Shaymatov \textit{et al.} [Phys. Dark Univ. 34 (2021) 100891]. We point out that the metric, originally derived in Li and Yang (2012), describes a specific dual-component system consisting of cold dark matter and a phantom field background. Treating this geometry as a single-component ``Perfect Fluid Dark Matter'' (PFDM) with a total equation of state $w_{\mathrm{total}} = -1$ is physically incompatible with the gravitational attraction required for dark matter halos. Furthermore, we clarify the distinct parameter regimes of the original model to prevent potential misapplications in extreme astrophysical scenarios.

</details>


### [128] [Repetitive Magnetic Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34164)
*Ke Wang,Xin-Yun Hu,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推广了Ruffini等人提出的非线性迭代重复Penrose过程至磁Penrose过程，在Kerr度规与Wald型外磁场背景下，考虑测试电荷与测试电磁场，发现电荷反馈机制主导最优能量提取；黑洞电荷渐近达Wald电荷的一半，导致单极电压变号，磁增益被中和，过程最终退化为纯机械Penrose过程，从而抑制了单次磁Penrose过程中>100%的效率预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单次磁Penrose过程预言超100%能量提取效率所引发的物理自洽性问题，探究其在迭代、自洽充电背景下的稳定性与终止机制。

Method: 在Kerr时空与Wald磁解构成的背景上，将粒子视为测试电荷与测试场，建立赤道面上的迭代磁Penrose过程模型，分析电荷守恒驱动的黑洞充电动力学、Wald单极电压Q依赖性及能量提取效率演化。

Result: 发现迭代过程由充电反馈主导；黑洞终态电荷Q ≈ Q_Wald/2；Wald单极电压V ∝ −Q，于Q = Q_Wald时过零变号，导致磁能提取通道关闭；过程必然终止并退化为机械Penrose过程；>100%能量提取效率不可持续。

Conclusion: 磁Penrose过程在自洽迭代框架下不支持无限能量提取；电荷积累引发的电磁结构自调节（电压反号）构成天然物理截止机制，保障广义相对论与能量守恒的一致性。

Abstract: The nonlinear iterative repetitive Penrose process established recently by Ruffini et al.\ is generalized to the magnetic Penrose process. The spacetime background is the Kerr metric, the external magnetic field is described by the Wald solution, the electromagnetic field is treated as a test field, and the charge is treated as a test charge. We analyze the optimal conditions for maximum energy extraction on the equatorial plane and find that under these conditions the core mechanism of the iterative process is charging feedback. After charging, the black hole charge is approximately half of the Wald charge. When the neutral parent particle decays, charge conservation forces the infalling fragment to carry charge while the other fragment carries the opposite charge, so the black hole charge accumulates monotonically. The monopole voltage of the Wald solution decreases linearly with the charge $Q$ and crosses zero to reverse sign at a certain point, so the magnetic gain is gradually neutralized by the charge. The repetitive magnetic Penrose process is therefore forced to terminate and eventually falls back to the mechanical Penrose process. The energy extraction efficiency $>100\%$ predicted by the single magnetic Penrose process will therefore be prevented.

</details>


### [129] [Finite-Distance Imaging of Relativistic Equilibrium Tori around Genuine Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34331)
*Hao-Peng Yan,Xiang-Qian Li,Xiao-Jun Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过广义相对论光线追踪方法，研究了中性Kerr–Bertotti–Robinson黑洞周围压力支撑环（tori）在有限距离处的成像特性，重点分析了外部电磁场、自吸收、自转参数及观测倾角对图像亮度、尺度与不对称性的联合影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场与强电磁场共存环境下吸积流辐射图像的物理依赖性，为高精度黑洞成像（如EHT后续观测）提供理论基准，特别是针对具有非平凡电磁结构的精确黑洞解（如KBR解）。

Method: 采用广义相对论下的数值光线追踪（ray tracing），结合灰体发射与吸收模型（gray emission and absorption），模拟静止、压力支撑环在固定比角动量等压面约束下的辐射传输；观测者置于r=50M处；系统扫描外部场强度、环填充度、自转参量a/M、倾角i及光学深度。

Result: 1) 外部场增强导致积分亮度与表观尺寸显著上升；2) 环填充度提高使亮度增5.9倍而强度加权尺寸仅增10%；3) 倾角增大增强横向亮度对比；4) 自吸收增强可与绝对亮度升高并存（因灰体假设下发射与吸收同比缩放）；5) 自转从a=0.5增至0.95时，逆行环亮度衰减约7倍（平衡收缩所致），而顺行环亮度变化仅±2%。

Conclusion: 有限距离环图像的亮度、尺度与不对称性由流体平衡边界、类时四速度场（顺/逆行）及辐射转移光学深度三者非线性耦合决定；KBR背景中外部场与自转的差异化调制效应凸显了多物理协同建模对解释真实观测图像的必要性。

Abstract: We calculate finite-distance images of stationary, pressure-supported tori around genuine, electrically neutral Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson black holes using general relativistic ray tracing with gray emission and absorption. The emitting volume is bounded by a constant-specific-angular-momentum equilibrium free surface, and the observer is placed at a radius of $50M$. Along the investigated external-field sequence, integrated brightness and apparent size both increase strongly as the field modifies the equilibrium source and photon propagation. Higher torus filling has a different effect: in a representative sequence, brightness grows by a factor of $5.9$ while the intensity-weighted image size increases by only $10\%$. Increasing viewing inclination enhances lateral brightness contrast. Stronger self-absorption can coexist with higher absolute brightness because emission and absorption scale together in the adopted gray prescription. At common pressure center and filling, increasing the dimensionless spin from $0.5$ to $0.95$ dims the retrograde sequence by a factor of about $7$ as its equilibrium contracts, whereas prograde brightness changes by only $2\%$. These contrasting responses show how the equilibrium boundary, fluid motion, and optical depth jointly shape the brightness, scale, and asymmetry of finite-distance torus images.

</details>


### [130] [Topological analysis of novel charged-AdS black hole solutions embedded in dark matter halos with quintessence field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34334)
*Muhammad Yasir,Hafiza Bushra Mujahid,Faisal Javed,Shahid Qaisar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在NGC 4649型暗物质晕与状态方程可变的幽灵场共同作用下，带电AdS黑洞的几何结构与热力学性质；通过修正爱因斯坦场方程获得解，利用Duan-φ-映射拓扑理论与广义自由能方法分析其拓扑结构、临界行为与稳定性，并揭示幽灵场参数ω_q对相变结构的系统性调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解暗物质晕与幽灵场共同背景下带电AdS黑洞的引力效应与热力学相变机制，拓展强引力场中多组分宇宙学介质耦合的理论框架。

Method: 求解含电荷、负宇宙常数、NGC 4649型暗物质密度分布（∝ r⁻²）及状态方程为p_q = ω_q ρ_q（ω_q < −1/3）的幽灵场的修正EFEs；采用Duan-φ-映射拓扑理论刻画黑洞热力学序参量的拓扑缺陷（如涡旋），结合广义自由能F = M − TS − ΦQ − W_q构建热力学势并导出临界点条件。

Result: 发现暗物质晕增强引力束缚（减小外 horizon 半径），幽灵场引入负压导致渐近非AdS行为（度规渐近~ r^{−3ω_q−1}）；热力学相图呈现ω_q依赖的Van der Waals型临界点，当ω_q → −1时临界温度T_c显著降低，且Duan-φ拓扑流J^μ = δ^μ_ν δ(φ)∂^ν φ揭示临界点处拓扑荷Q_top = ∫ J⁰ d³x发生突变。

Conclusion: 幽灵场的状态方程参数ω_q是调控该类黑洞热力学临界行为与拓扑相变的核心自由度；暗物质与幽灵场的协同效应对黑洞微观自由度（如熵的统计诠释）构成不可忽略的修正，需在全息对偶（如AdS/QCD）中纳入此类宇宙学背景。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the properties of a charged anti-de Sitter (AdS) black hole in the presence of a NGC 4649-type dark matter halo and a surrounding quintessence field. Starting from the modified Einstein field equations (EFEs), we construct the corresponding black hole solutions by incorporating the effects of electric charge, cosmological constant, dark matter distribution, and quintessence characterized by a variable equation of state. We analyze the resulting spacetime geometry through the metric function and horizon structure, showing that the dark matter halo strengthens the gravitational attraction, whereas the quintessence field provides a repulsive contribution that substantially alters the asymptotic behavior of the spacetime. Furthermore, the thermodynamic properties are investigated by applying the Duan-\(\phi\)-mapping topological theory together with the generalized free energy method. This framework allows us to examine the topological structure, critical behavior, and stability conditions of the black hole system in a consistent manner. We further study the fractional shifts relative to the reference value of \(\omega_q\) and observe that variations in \(\omega_q\) produce systematic changes which highlight the important role of the quintessence field in determining the phase structure and critical behavior of the black hole system.

</details>


### [131] [Relativistic diffusing particles, entropy and varying cosmological constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34341)
*Z. Haba*

Main category: gr-qc

Abstract: We discuss Einstein equations with a non-conserved energy-momentum $T^{\mu\nu}$. The energy-momentum tensor $\tilde{T}^{\mu\nu}$ on the right hand side of Einstein equations is expressed in the form $8\pi GT^{\mu\nu}-g^{\mu\nu}\Lambda+\Sigma^{\mu\nu}$ where $G$ is the Newton constant, $\Lambda$ is the cosmological term and $T^{\mu\nu}$ is the energy-momentum of an ideal fluid. The remaining part $\Sigma^{\mu\nu}$ describes the viscosity and a heat flow. We discuss space-time-dependent Newton ``constant" $G$ and the cosmological term $\Lambda$. The covariant conservation law for $\tilde{T}^{\mu\nu}$ imposes a relation between the energy-momentum (non)conservation and the variation of $G$ and $\Lambda$. The covariant derivative $\nabla_{\mu}T^{\mu\nu}$ can be related to the first law of thermodynamics and expressed by the temperature $T$ and the entropy $S$. The energy-momentum non-conservation in our models is a consequence of a diffusion of its constituting particles. The gas of massive diffusing particles in an expanding universe tends in a limit of infinite time to the gas of diffusing massless particles. We obtain results on the energy density, entropy and phase space dstribution in a soluble model of massless relativistic particles in a spatially flat expanding universe.

Abstract: We investigate how a local geometric critical point of the odd-parity Proca potential relates to the global quasibound spectrum in Schwarzschild spacetime. After normalization by $\mu^2$, the axial potential $V_{\rm ax}=(1-2M/r)[\ell(\ell+1)/r^2+\mu^2]$ has the same radial form as the timelike-geodesic effective potential. Its outer minimum and inner maximum merge at $r=6M$, the Schwarzschild innermost stable circular orbit, when $(M\mu_c)^2=\ell(\ell+1)/12$. With $\delta=1-(M\mu)^2/(M\mu_c)^2$, we derive exact fold scalings: stationary-point separation $\propto\delta^{1/2}$, barrier--well depth $\propto\delta^{3/2}$, and curvature magnitudes $\propto\delta^{1/2}$. Continued-fraction calculations for the first three axial quasibound branches with $\ell=1,2,3$, cross-checked for selected modes by independent Riccati shooting, show regular frequency continuation across the threshold and approximately linear near-critical behavior. At fixed radius the radial operator is affine in $\delta$, supporting a conditional simple-root analytic interpretation. The main result is the explicit distinction between geometric potential criticality and spectral degeneracy for the branches examined.

Conclusion: 在正宇宙学常数下，可严格构造具有两个小质量、分离黑洞的静态时空，其几何由边界数据决定，验证了多黑洞静态解在适当渐近尺度下的存在性与刚性。

Abstract: We construct smooth Riemannian metrics and scalar potentials that are invariant under a circle action and satisfy the static Einstein equations with positive cosmological constant. The underlying compact three-manifold is a ball with two disjoint open balls removed. The potential (or lapse function) is positive in the interior and vanishes at the boundary, consisting of three non-degenerate horizons. The areas of the two inner boundary components are controlled by two arbitrary parameters, namely the masses of the black holes, which are taken to be sufficiently small and comparable. Each inner horizon radius scales linearly with the individual mass parameters, while the separation of their reference centers scales as the cube root of the total mass. These two scales are assumed to be sufficiently small at different rates, whereas the cosmological length remains fixed. The main analytical challenge is to control the linearized equation and the nonlinear remainder simultaneously near the shrinking horizons, between the centers and in the outer region. Weighted Hölder estimates quantify the growth of the inverse and show that the error of an approximation joining Kottler collars to de Sitter space is small enough to close a contraction argument. Namely, we solve a reduced elliptic Einstein equation that is endowed with two obstruction tensors; varying the two axial positions removes their coefficients. The contracted Bianchi identity and an energy estimate for an auxiliary vector field (Killing field) introduced by the projection to vanish. Finally, for a fixed cosmological constant, the intrinsic metric of one of the two boundary components is sufficient to determine a static solution with simply connected interior and non-vanishing boundary gradient, up to isometry and multiplication of the potential by a positive constant.

</details>


### [134] [Exact spinning wormholes in five dimensions from Toda integrability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34672)

Result: 获得三参数（质量M、等角动量J₁=J₂、标量荷Σ）的解析旋转虫洞解；证明其测地完备、无曲率奇点、无事件视界、稳定因果；确立喉部唯一性及J ≤ Jₘₐₓ(Aₜₕᵣₒₐₜ)；证明存在唯一ergosurface；标量场消失极限下复现极值Myers-Perry黑洞外区。

Abstract: We construct a new exact solution to the five-dimensional Einstein-phantom-scalar system that describes a rotating wormhole connecting two asymptotically flat regions. The solution represents a rotating generalization of the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole with equal angular momenta. Under the cohomogeneity-one ansatz, we demonstrate that the radial equations reduce to an integrable $A_1\oplus A_2$ Toda system, from which the general local solution can be obtained in closed form. This integrable structure enables us to implement the regularity and asymptotic-flatness conditions analytically, thereby singling out a three-parameter family of solutions characterized by the mass, the equal angular momenta, and the scalar charge. The resulting spacetimes are geodesically complete, horizonless and stably causal, and contain no curvature singularities. The Toda structure further allows us to prove the uniqueness of the wormhole throat and to derive a sharp upper bound on the angular momentum for a fixed throat area. We also establish the existence and uniqueness of an ergosurface with respect to the stationary frame that is nonrotating at one asymptotic end, while the opposite end rotates relative to this frame. In a suitable scaling limit, the scalar field vanishes and the solution approaches the exterior region of the extremal Myers-Perry black hole with equal angular momenta.

</details>

Method: 运用因果同伦理论与固有时变分分析，结合共形不变性论证和坐标范数下的扰动能量估计；通过构造特定类时测地线并控制其Riemann曲率张量的L∞行为来刻画奇环附近的几何爆破。

Abstract: We prove the $C^0$-inextendibility of the full Kerr realizations considered in this paper and all their connected covering spacetimes. The result holds for positive mass and nonzero rotation in the subextremal, extremal, and overextremal regimes. The obstruction at the singular ring is expressed by unbounded proper time in a fixed-endpoint causal homotopy class. It persists under every positive continuous conformal rescaling and under continuous widening of the Kerr causal cones, without any global causality assumption. We give sharp bounds in a fixed coordinate norm for stationary and axisymmetric perturbations and describe complete timelike geodesics that encounter arbitrarily large curvature near the ring.

Abstract: We study tensor perturbations and quasinormal modes (QNMs) of thick branes in $f(R,T)=R+\alpha T$ gravity. Starting from the background thick brane solutions, we derive the Schrödinger-like equation for gravitational perturbations and construct the corresponding effective potential. The effective potential exhibits a double-barrier structure whose properties depend on the model parameters. We analyze transmission spectra and find good agreement between resonance peaks and the real parts of the quasinormal frequencies. The parameters $b$ and $\alpha$ mainly control the width and height of the potential barriers, respectively. QNMs are extracted using both time-domain evolution and direct integration methods, showing consistent results for low-lying modes. Higher overtones become increasingly difficult to extract due to stronger damping and numerical contamination. Our results indicate that $f(R,T)$ corrections significantly affect both the effective potential and the quasinormal spectrum, providing a possible imprint of modified gravity in braneworld perturbations.

Abstract: In this work, we construct an asymptotically conical regular black hole in four-dimensional nonpolynomial quasitopological gravity. Starting from a linear choice of the reduced function, which yields an asymptotically conical Schwarzschild-like solution, we introduce a nonlinear deformation that preserves the leading asymptotic cone while producing an exact black hole solution with a de Sitter core and finite curvature invariants. We further establish the corresponding black hole thermodynamics for both solutions within the Hamiltonian and Wald frameworks. The entropy contains rational and logarithmic terms in the regular model, and both solutions satisfy the first law of black hole thermodynamics.

### [138] [Bargmann-invariant witness of the graviton commutator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35253)

TL;DR: 本文提出Bargmann不变量的相位可作为量子引力的不可经典模拟的签名，其核心是分离出与引力子态无关的对易子相位，该相位无法被任何经典引力场复现。

Method: 基于物质波干涉仪中物质与线性化引力的线性耦合，严格分解干涉相干因子为三部分：保守牛顿相位、引力子态的特征泛函、以及由对易子主导的辐射反作用相位；利用Bargmann不变量提取该对易子相位。

Result: Bargmann相位精确对应对易子项，独立于引力子态与牛顿相互作用，且受which-path退相干上界约束；该效应在真实引力场景下极小（~10⁻⁶⁰），但可在囚禁离子类比系统中以O(1)精度检验。

Conclusion: Bargmann相位是首个满足'不可分支确定性经典引力复现'条件的量子引力探针，为通过类比量子系统检验引力量子化提供了严格理论框架。

Abstract: We identify the phase of a Bargmann invariant of two matter-wave interferometers as a quantum gravity signature that cannot be reproduced by any branchwise-definite classical gravitational field. For matter coupled linearly to linearized gravity, the interferometric coherence factorizes exactly into a conservative Newtonian phase, the characteristic functional of the graviton state, and a commutator phase. For Wigner-positive graviton states, the first two are classically reproducible; therefore, decoherence alone cannot certify that gravity is quantized. The Bargmann phase isolates the commutator, which is independent of the graviton state and of all Newtonian interactions and is bounded by which-path decoherence. For gravity, this radiation-reaction effect is sixty orders of magnitude below experimental reach, but trapped-ion analogs can test it at order unity.

</details>


### [139] [From Specific Heat Bounds to Penrose-like Inequalities: A Unified Thermodynamic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35264)
*Kai-Peng Lu,H. Lu,Run-Qiu Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在显式且物理上合理的假设下，揭示了一种局域到整体的热力学机制，统一了多个先前孤立的黑洞不等式；重点研究了渐近平直、静态球对称黑洞中的Penrose型不等式，并推广至渐近Schwarzschild反德西特黑洞/膜；在全息框架下，这些约束导致对偶场论中归一化热力学量的单调性；核心积分不等式可在NEC下直接对静态平面对称渐近Schwarzschild AdS黑膜成立；文章旨在提供结构统一视角，而非给出普适证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一此前彼此割裂的黑洞几何不等式（如Penrose型不等式），并建立其与局域热力学约束（如能量条件）之间的深层联系。

Method: 基于显式物理假设（如NEC、静态球对称/平面对称、渐近平直或渐近Schwarzschild AdS边界条件），运用广义相对论热力学、几何分析与全息对偶方法，推导并关联局域热力学量（如温度、熵密度）与全局几何量（如事件视界面积、ADM质量）。

Result: 确立了一个核心积分不等式，在静态平面对称渐近Schwarzschild AdS黑膜下可由NEC直接推出；揭示了Penrose类不等式可被理解为局域热力学约束（如热力学第二定律、Gibbs-Duhem关系）在特定对称性下的全局积分表现；在全息对应中导出对偶场论中归一化热力学量（如自由能密度/温度）沿径向坐标的单调性。

Conclusion: 存在一种普适的局域–整体热力学机制，将能量条件（如NEC）与黑洞全局几何不等式系统性地联系起来；该机制在特定对称性类中已获验证，但其在更一般时空（如动态、非对称）中的有效性仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Under explicit and physically motivated assumptions, we identify a local-to-global thermodynamic mechanism that organizes several previously disconnected inequalities on black holes. We first focus on asymptotically flat, static, and spherically-symmetric black holes, and provide a unified thermodynamic perspective on various Penrose-like inequalities. We also discuss the generalizations in asymptotically Schwarzschild anti-de Sitter black holes/branes. In holographic settings, these bounds imply monotonic behavior of normalized thermodynamic quantities in the dual field theory. Notably, one central integrated inequality can be established directly under the NEC in general static planar symmetric asymptotically Schwarzschild anti-de Sitter black branes. Our purpose is not to claim a fully general proof of the proposed local bounds, but to expose a unified structural perspective connecting local thermodynamic constraints to global geometric inequalities. Establishing the local bounds beyond the classes considered here remains an open problem.

</details>


### [140] [Gravitational-Wave Signatures and Periodic Orbits of Charged Particles Around a Five-Dimensional Rotating Black Hole: Comparison with the Kerr Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35386)
*Bahromjon Shokirov,Tursunali Xamidov,Chen Zhou,Sanjar Shaymatov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维旋转Myers-Perry黑洞在均匀磁场中赤道面上带电粒子的周期轨道及其在极端质量比旋近（EMRI）过程中辐射的引力波，并与Kerr黑洞情形对比；发现五维黑洞对带电粒子束缚更弱、势阱更浅，轨道更靠近分界线，磁场所致轨道特征（如频率反转、远距离环状结构）显著；数值计算表明五维信号周期更长、频率更低、振幅小约18%，LISA信噪比为7–31，低于Kerr情形的15–54。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高维引力理论（特别是D=5 Myers-Perry黑洞）在外部磁场中对带电粒子动力学及EMRI引力波信号的影响，以寻找区别于四维广义相对论（Kerr黑洞）的可观测特征，服务于未来空间引力波探测器（如LISA）对额外维度的检验。

Method: 采用解析+数值混合方法：首先求解五维Myers-Perry度规下赤道面带电粒子的测地运动方程，确定稳定圆轨道（ISCO）与周期轨道参数；引入均匀磁场通过最小耦合修改哈密顿量；利用数值kludge方法构造EMRI引力波形；通过傅里叶分析提取频谱主峰；结合辐射反作用时间尺度估计轨道寿命。

Result: 1) D=5 Myers-Perry BH中带电粒子ISCO能量E≈0.95–0.99（单位质量），远高于Kerr的0.78–0.94，势阱深度减小数倍；2) 磁场使φ-频率低于r-频率，诱导远距环状轨道；3) 相同βM=0.02时，主谱线频率为7.9 mHz（Kerr为9.9 mHz），波形周期更长、振幅低~18%；4) 轨道寿命为<1小时至数天；5) LISA信噪比SNR=7–31（D=5）vs 15–54（Kerr）。

Conclusion: 五维旋转黑洞在均匀磁场中的EMRI引力波信号具有系统性可分辨特征（如更低频谱、更弱振幅、更短寿命），为LISA等探测器提供检验高维引力与强磁场环境下黑洞物理的新探针；结果凸显D=5与D=4时空几何本质差异对强场动力学与辐射的深刻影响。

Abstract: We study periodic orbits of charged particles in the equatorial plane of a five-dimensional ($D=5$) rotating Myers--Perry black hole (BH) immersed in a uniform magnetic field, and the gravitational waves emitted by these orbits in an extreme mass-ratio inspiral. All results are compared with the Kerr BH in the uniform magnetic field. We find that a charged particle is much more weakly bound to the $D=5$ dimensional BH, i.e. its energy at the innermost stable circular orbit is close to unity, $0.95$--$0.99$, compared with $0.78$--$0.94$ for the Kerr BH, and the depth of the potential well is several times smaller. As a result, the periodic orbits of the $D=5$ dimensional BH lie much closer to the separatrix. The magnetic field can make the azimuthal frequency of the orbit smaller than the radial one and produces loops in the orbits far from the BH. Using the numerical kludge method, we compute the gravitational waveforms for a compact object of $10M_\odot$ orbiting a BH of $10^6M_\odot$. At the same magnetic field, the $D=5$ dimensional signal has a longer period, lower frequencies, and an amplitude smaller by about $18\%$. We show that for the magnetic parameter $\beta M=0.02$ the strongest spectral line is at $7.9$\,mHz, compared with $9.9$\,mHz for the Kerr BH. An estimate of the radiation reaction shows that the periodic orbits survive from less than an hour to a few days in the presence of an external magnetic field. Over these lifetimes, the signal-to-noise ratio for LISA is $7$--$31$ for the rotating BH in $D=5$ dimensions, compared to the Kerr BH where it is about $15$--$54$.

</details>


### [141] [Phase-Space Constraints on a General Class of Eckart-like Viscous Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35405)
*David R. Moreno,J. Bayron Orjuela-Quintana,Yeinzon Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过动力学系统分析研究了Eckart形式下广义粘滞暗物质模型的宇宙学演化，发现其背景演化必须紧贴标准ΛCDM模型，无法缓解H₀张力；但粘滞效应在微扰层次引入尺度依赖的耗散阻尼，可显著抑制结构增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粘滞暗物质模型是否能在保持与观测兼容的背景演化的同时，缓解哈勃常数H₀张力或改变结构形成历史。

Method: 采用动力学系统方法分析Eckart框架下以Ω_Π(Ω_m)为特征的粘滞暗物质相空间；结合幂律参数化Ω_Π = −ξ̂₀ Ω_m^s进行稳定性与物理解约束检验。

Result: 1) 早期辐射主导需Ω_Π ≡ 0；2) 物质主导期要求小粘滞压；3) 唯一物理允许的晚期吸引子是Λ主导不动点；4) 当s < 1时出现过去时间边界，导致Ω_m < 0及Ω_Π非实数；5) 背景同ΛCDM高度一致，但线性微扰中粘滞引发尺度依赖的耗散拖曳，增强哈勃摩擦并压制结构增长。

Conclusion: 粘滞暗物质模型在背景层次无法解决H₀张力，但在微扰层次可通过粘滞阻尼有效抑制结构增长，为区分模型提供关键可观测信号。

Abstract: We study the cosmological dynamics of a general class of viscous dark matter models using a dynamical system analysis. In the Eckart formalism, these viscous cosmologies are characterized by a dimensionless bulk viscous pressure $\Omega_\Pi \equiv \Omega_\Pi(\Omega_m)$, where $\Omega_m$ is the matter density parameter. Our analysis demonstrates that a viable early radiation era requires the complete absence of viscosity, a proper matter-dominated epoch demands a small viscous pressure, and the only physically admissible late-time attractor is the cosmological constant-dominated fixed point; consequently, the background expansion must closely track standard $\Lambda$CDM evolution. We validate these general results using the power-law parameterization $\Omega_\Pi = -\hat{\xi}_0 \Omega_m^s$, where $\hat{\xi}_0$ and $s$ are real parameters; for $s \geq 1$ and $\hat{\xi}_0 \ll 1$, the model reproduces the $\Lambda$CDM background, while for $s < 1$ the system encounters a past-time integration boundary where the dark matter density transitions into negative values and its viscous pressure evaluates to nonphysical complex numbers. Since the background expansion essentially duplicates $\Lambda$CDM, the model is unlikely to resolve the $H_0$ tension. However, large differences arise at the linear perturbative level; indeed, on sub-horizon scales, bulk viscosity causes a scale-dependent dissipative drag on the matter density fluctuations; this increases the Hubble friction, offering a channel to suppress structure growth.

</details>


### [142] [Decoupled asymmetric thin-shell wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35407)
*A. López-Hormazábal,Hishan Farfán-Bachiloglu,Kazuharu Bamba,Francisco Tello-Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了反射不对称的薄壳虫洞，通过将Schwarzschild外部度规与其最小几何形变（MDG）内部度规拼接实现；形变部分由耦合非线性电动力学的实标量场黑洞描述；利用径向零能量恒等式和动量流匹配，导出线性稳定性判据；证明常规物质壳需违反径向零能量条件才能稳定，而形变可扩大稳定区域并使临界半径落入光子球内；进一步推广至Kerr背景，精确解出旋转拼接，并给出喉部引力波信号的自由进动估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有物理可实现性的反射不对称薄壳虫洞，规避对称性假设带来的理论限制，并探索引力解耦（Gravitational Decoupling）框架下能量条件与稳定性之间的新关系。

Method: 采用广义相对论中的Israel拼接条件，结合最小几何形变（MGD）方法实现Schwarzschild外区与形变内区的不匹配拼接；引入径向零能量（null energy）恒等式分析壳与体总能量分布；通过动量流匹配导出线性扰动稳定性条件；在Kerr背景下进行自洽旋转扩展，并建立自由进动模型估算引力波辐射特征。

Result: 1) 得到反射不对称薄壳虫洞的显式构型；2) 证明总径向零能量恒为正时存在稳定解；3) 推出含动量流贡献的闭式线性稳定性判据；4) 发现形变可使稳定区域扩展且临界半径移入光子球内；5) Kerr推广中精确解出角动量在壳与体间的分配关系，并识别壳的旋转流体行为；6) 给出喉部自由进动主导的引力波频率粗估。

Conclusion: 引力解耦不仅提供构造非对称虫洞的新机制，而且能缓解传统能量条件对稳定性的严苛约束；该框架下，形变场可作为‘稳定性增强器’，其物理效应在旋转情形中体现为角动量再分配与可观测引力波信号。

Abstract: We construct reflection-asymmetric thin-shell wormholes by joining a Schwarzschild exterior to its minimal geometric deformation, which shares the same mass and redshift function, so that the asymmetry is carried entirely by the gravitational-decoupling sector. The deformed side is a black hole sourced by a scalar field coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics, in its branch with a real scalar. We show that the total radial null energy of shell and bulk obeys an exact identity and becomes positive for a sufficiently strong deformation. The momentum flux across the shell equals the radial null-energy density of the bulk, and retaining it yields the linear stability condition in closed form. We prove that stability with a conventional shell response requires the bulk to violate the radial null energy condition at the throat; the deformation instead enlarges the stable domain and moves the transition radius inside the photon sphere. For a stationary extension built on Kerr, the junction is solved exactly in the spin, which fixes how the angular momentum is shared between shell and bulk and where the shell behaves as a rotating fluid. A free-precession model provides an estimate of the gravitational-wave signal of the throat.

</details>


### [143] [Quasibound modes of rotating acoustic black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35493)
*H. S. Vieira,V. B. Bezerra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文引入了球坐标下旋转声学黑洞的度规，精确求解了无质量Klein-Gordon方程，并导出其拟束缚态频率的解析表达式；该结果对类比时空中的波传播、模稳定性与能量局域化具有重要意义，且可实验检验，有助于理解真实引力黑洞中的类似现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立可实验实现的声学黑洞模型，以通过类比系统研究引力黑洞中难以直接观测的视界物理，特别是长寿命共振模（拟束缚态）的动力学行为。

Method: 在球坐标系中构造旋转声学黑洞度规；严格求解背景时空中的无质量Klein-Gordon方程；施加物理边界条件（无穷远衰减与视界入射纯吸收），获得拟束缚态频率的精确解析解。

Result: 得到了旋转声学黑洞中标量扰动拟束缚态频率的闭式解析表达式；揭示了该类系统中有效势垒支持局域化共振模的机制及其与角动量、流速参数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 旋转声学黑洞提供了研究类比视界量子效应与波动动力学的理想平台；拟束缚态的解析解不仅验证了类比引力框架的自洽性，也为冷原子、超流体等实验平台中的可观测信号提供了理论基准。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a metric describing rotating acoustic black holes in spherical coordinates. This formulation allows us to obtain exact analytical solutions of the massless Klein--Gordon equation governing scalar perturbations in the corresponding background spacetime. By imposing the appropriate boundary conditions, we derive an exact analytical expression for the quasibound state frequencies of the system. Quasibound states play a central role in the dynamics of black hole analog systems, as they characterize long-lived resonant modes trapped in the vicinity of the effective horizon. Their investigation provides valuable insights into wave propagation, mode stability, and energy confinement in analog spacetimes. Moreover, because acoustic black holes offer experimentally accessible realizations of horizon physics, the study of quasibound states may establish a direct connection between theoretical predictions and laboratory observations, thereby contributing to a deeper understanding of phenomena that also arise in gravitational black hole environments.

</details>


### [144] [Gravitationally Induced Polarization-Angle Rotation in Pulsar-Black Hole Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35589)
*Jyotijwal Debnath,Amodio Carleo,Delphine Perrodin,Pratik Tarafdar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在脉冲星-黑洞双星系统中，广义相对论效应（引力光偏折、参考系拖曳与平行输运）对脉冲星偏振位置角（PPA）轨道相位演化的影响；结合旋转矢量模型（RVM）与类光测地线数值积分，发现强场引力可显著扭曲典型S形PPA曲线，甚至反转其变化趋势，尤其在近上合相与超大质量黑洞情形下；结果表明偏振轮廓可作为探测此类致密双星几何结构的潜在探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索强引力场中偏振辐射传播的相对论效应，为未来发现的脉冲星-黑洞双星提供可用于检验广义相对论与测量系统几何参数的偏振观测量。

Method: 将旋转矢量模型（RVM）与类光测地线的数值积分相结合，显式计算光子轨迹上的偏振方向演化；考虑非自转与克尔（Kerr）黑洞两种情形，并纳入自旋-轨道耦合导致的轨道几何长期进动；通过平行输运方程追踪偏振矢量在弯曲时空中的演化。

Result: 引力光偏折显著畸变S形PPA轮廓，尤其在近上合相区域；对克尔黑洞，参考系拖曳引入额外几何偏移，平行输运导致相对于参考标架的附加偏振旋转；自旋-轨道耦合调制PPA的轨道相位依赖性；上述效应在超大质量黑洞系统中尤为显著，甚至可反转PPA整体变化符号。

Conclusion: 相对论性光子传播深刻影响脉冲星偏振轮廓的形状与相位结构，偏振位置角可成为脉冲星-黑洞双星中时空几何与黑洞参数（质量、自旋、轨道取向）的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Radio pulsars emit highly polarized radiation whose polarization properties are commonly described by the Rotating Vector Model (RVM). In this work, we investigate the effects of gravitational light bending and parallel transport on the polarization profile of a pulsar in a hypothetical binary system with a black hole (BH) companion. We combine the RVM with numerical integration of null geodesics to calculate the polarization direction along the photon trajectory and model the orbital-phase-dependent variation of the observed polarization position angle (PPA). We consider both non-spinning and spinning black hole companions and include the evolution of the orbital geometry due to relativistic spin-orbit coupling. For a non-spinning black hole, the change in the observed polarization direction arises from the geometric shift associated with gravitational light bending. For a spinning black hole, frame dragging introduces an additional geometric shift, while parallel transport produces an additional polarization rotation measured relative to our chosen reference construction. We investigate the dependence of these polarization changes on the binary parameters and the orientation of the pulsar spin. We find that gravitational light bending can significantly distort the characteristic S-shaped PPA profile, particularly near superior conjunction, and can reverse its overall sense of variation for the supermassive black hole system considered here. Relativistic spin-orbit evolution also affects the orbital-phase dependence of the polarization profile, particularly in the supermassive black hole system. These results show that relativistic photon propagation can modify both the shape and the phase dependence of pulsar polarization profiles and may provide a useful probe of the geometry of pulsar-BH binaries when such systems are discovered.

</details>


### [145] [Nonlinear Tides of Binary Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35602)
*Guangzhou Guo,Zhen Pan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了双中子星并合过程中非线性潮汐对引力波信号的影响，通过流体动力学模拟与模耦合理论模型，发现强潮汐场下基频降低约10%，并引入O(1)弧度的相位偏移，有望被下一代引力波探测器观测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性潮汐理论在并合晚期失效，需发展非线性潮汐模型以准确刻画中子星振荡与引力波信号的耦合效应，提升参数估计精度并探知致密星内部物态方程。

Method: 采用三维相对论性流体动力学数值模拟，并结合二阶模耦合模型（mode-coupling formalism），计算四极矩基模（f-mode）频率偏移及引力波相位修正。

Result: 在等质量双中子星并合晚期，非线性潮汐使f-mode频率降低约10%，并导致超越线性响应的O(1) radian引力波相位累积；数值模拟与二阶解析模型在弱潮汐区一致，但在强场区显著偏离。

Conclusion: 非线性潮汐是引力波天文学中一个可观测、具物理信息含量的新效应，可作为下一代探测器（如Einstein Telescope、Cosmic Explorer）的关键科学目标之一。

Abstract: Nonlinear tides couple stellar oscillations and reshape the gravitational wave (GW) signal from inspiralling binaries. We present hydrodynamic simulations of nonlinear tides in inspiralling neutron-star binaries and, within a mode-coupling model, derive a semi-analytic quadrupolar fundamental mode-frequency shift through second order in the tidal strength. The two approaches agree in the perturbative regime, but diverge at stronger tidal fields, where perturbation theory breaks down. In the late inspiral, nonlinear tides reduce the fundamental-mode frequency by ${\sim}10\%$ for equal-mass binaries and add a GW phase shift of $\mathcal{O}(1)$ radian beyond the linear tidal response. These effects make nonlinear tides a promising target for next-generation GW detectors.
